```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 9j. Deploy: the deployments table, collection_record_deploy, the guard's lock branch and
// collection_placeholder_ready (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 4)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
type DeploymentRow = {
  collection_id: string
  chain_id: number
  factory: string
  deployer: string
  salt: string
  code_hash: string
  contract_address: string
  tx_hash: string
  base_uri: string
  payout_address: string
  royalty_bps: number
  royalty_receiver: string
  public_max_per_wallet: number
  mint_fee_flat_wei: string
  mint_fee_bps: number
  airdrop_fee_per_token_wei: string
  deployed_at: string
}

const DEPLOYMENT_COLUMNS =
  'collection_id, chain_id, factory, deployer, salt, code_hash, contract_address, tx_hash, base_uri, payout_address, royalty_bps, royalty_receiver, public_max_per_wallet, mint_fee_flat_wei::text, mint_fee_bps, airdrop_fee_per_token_wei::text, deployed_at'

async function deploymentOf(client: SupabaseClient, cid: string) {
  const { data, error } = await client
    .from('deployments')
    .select(DEPLOYMENT_COLUMNS)
    .eq('collection_id', cid)
    .maybeSingle()
  return { row: data as unknown as DeploymentRow | null, error: error as DbError }
}

type DeployedState = {
  status: string
  chain_id: number | null
  contract_address: string | null
  slug: string | null
  slug_reserved_at: string | null
  art_locked_at: string | null
}

async function deployedState(cid: string): Promise<DeployedState> {
  const { data, error } = await admin
    .from('collections')
    .select('status, chain_id, contract_address, slug, slug_reserved_at, art_locked_at')
    .eq('id', cid)
    .single()
  if (error) throw new Error(`deployed state: ${describe(error as DbError)}`)
  return data as DeployedState
}

type GroupRow = { onchain_id: number; name: string; price_wei: string; max_supply: number; start_at: string | null }

async function groupsOf(cid: string): Promise<GroupRow[]> {
  const { data, error } = await admin
    .from('mint_groups')
    .select('onchain_id, name, price_wei::text, max_supply, start_at')
    .eq('collection_id', cid)
    .order('onchain_id')
  if (error) throw new Error(`groups: ${describe(error as DbError)}`)
  return data as unknown as GroupRow[]
}

/** Takes a paid (or placeholder) job through its one batch to done, as the worker would. */
async function finishRender(
  jobId: string,
  imagesCid: string,
  metadataCid: string,
  baseUri: string,
  placeholderImageCid: string | null = null,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const { failed } = await runSteps(
    [
      ['art_claim_render', { p_job_id: jobId }],
      ['art_batch_start', { p_job_id: jobId, p_index: 0 }],
      ['art_batch_done', { p_job_id: jobId, p_index: 0, p_images_cid: imagesCid, p_images_size: 1000 }],
      [
        'art_render_finish',
        {
          p_job_id: jobId,
          p_metadata_cid: metadataCid,
          p_base_uri: baseUri,
          p_placeholder_image_cid: placeholderImageCid,
        },
      ],
    ],
    async () => '',
  )
  return failed
}

/** A generative collection of three tokens, locked, paid and rendered to done; with a live link unless `link` is false. */
async function renderedFixture(A: Creator, payer: string, tag: number, options: { link?: boolean } = {}) {
  const { cid, jobId, list } = await renderFixture(A, payer, tag)
  const failed = await finishRender(jobId, CID('d'), CID('e'), `ipfs://${CID('e')}/`)
  if (failed) throw new Error(`setup: render: ${failed}`)
  if (options.link !== false) {
    const error = await reserve(A.client, cid, slug())
    if (error) throw new Error(`setup: link: ${describe(error)}`)
  }
  return { cid, jobId, list }
}

/** A collection that finishes its art later, its placeholder locked and rendered to done, with a live link; a supply unless `withSupply` is false. */
async function deferredFixture(A: Creator, withSupply = true) {
  const col = await insertCollection(A.client)
  if (!col.row) throw new Error(`setup: ${describe(col.error)}`)
  const cid = col.row.id
  const set = await A.client
    .from('collections')
    .update({ art_deferred: true, ...(withSupply ? { max_supply: 5 } : {}) })
    .eq('id', cid)
  if (set.error) throw new Error(`setup: deferred: ${describe(set.error as DbError)}`)
  const upload = await recordUpload(A.client, cid, { kind: 'placeholder', file_name: 'placeholder.png' })
  if (upload.error || !upload.id) throw new Error(`setup: placeholder upload: ${describe(upload.error)}`)
  const lock = await callArt(A.client, 'art_lock_placeholder', {
    p_collection_id: cid,
    p_placeholder_upload_id: upload.id,
  })
  if (lock.error) throw new Error(`setup: placeholder lock: ${describe(lock.error)}`)
  const jobId = idOf(lock.data)
  const failed = await finishRender(jobId, CID('p'), CID('q'), `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json`, CID('p'))
  if (failed) throw new Error(`setup: placeholder render: ${failed}`)
  const link = await reserve(A.client, cid, slug())
  if (link) throw new Error(`setup: link: ${describe(link)}`)
  return { cid, jobId }
}

const ADDRESS40 = (n: number) => `0x${n.toString(16).padStart(40, '0')}`

/** The record function's arguments for a collection, every address lowercase, every wei a string. */
const recordArgs = (
  owner: string,
  cid: string,
  payer: string,
  n: number,
  overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {},
): Record<string, unknown> => ({
  p_collection_id: cid,
  p_owner: owner,
  p_chain_id: 31337,
  p_factory: FACTORY_ADDRESS,
  p_deployer: payer,
  p_salt: `0x${HEX64(n)}`,
  p_code_hash: `0x${HEX64(1000 + n)}`,
  p_args: {
    name: 'Verify',
    symbol: 'VFY',
    maxSupply: '3',
    groupSupplies: ['2', '1'],
    groupPrices: ['0', '1000000000000000'],
  },
  p_contract_address: ADDRESS40(n),
  p_tx_hash: `0x${HEX64(2000 + n)}`,
  p_base_uri: `ipfs://${CID('e')}/`,
  p_payout_address: payer,
  p_royalty_bps: 500,
  p_royalty_receiver: payer,
  p_public_max_per_wallet: 3,
  p_mint_fee_flat_wei: '0',
  p_mint_fee_bps: 0,
  p_airdrop_fee_per_token_wei: '0',
  p_groups: [
    { name: 'Public', price_wei: '0', max_supply: 2 },
    { name: 'Holders', price_wei: '1000000000000000', max_supply: 1 },
  ],
  ...overrides,
})

const BIG_FEE = '8000000000000000000123'
const LOCK_SENTENCE = 'The name, symbol and description cannot change while the art is locked.'

async function artDeploy(A: Creator, B: Creator): Promise<void> {
  const payer = A.account.address.toLowerCase()
  // Rendered, linked: the deployment that lands.
  const rendered = await renderedFixture(A, payer, 141)
  // Rendered, no link, then a lapsed link: refusal (2).
  const unlinked = await renderedFixture(A, payer, 142, { link: false })
  // Deferred with its placeholder done: refusal (5) with the first deployment's hash, then its own deployment.
  const deferred = await deferredFixture(A)
  // Deferred without a supply: refusal (3).
  const bare = await deferredFixture(A, false)
  // A draft with a supply and a link but nothing rendered: refusal (4).
  const draft = await insertCollection(A.client)
  if (!draft.row) throw new Error(`setup: ${describe(draft.error)}`)
  const draftId = draft.row.id
  // Locked, waiting for payment: the guard's lock branch.
  const locked = await renderFixture(A, payer, 146, false)

  await check('deployments: the owner reads nothing before deploy, and a client insert is refused', async () => {
    const read = await A.client.from('deployments').select('collection_id')
    if (read.error) return describe(read.error as DbError)
    if (read.data.length > 0) return `${read.data.length} row(s) before any deploy`
    const { data, error } = await A.client
      .from('deployments')
      .insert({
        collection_id: rendered.cid,
        chain_id: 31337,
        factory: FACTORY_ADDRESS,
        deployer: payer,
        salt: `0x${HEX64(1)}`,
        code_hash: `0x${HEX64(1001)}`,
        args: {},
        contract_address: ADDRESS40(1),
        tx_hash: `0x${HEX64(2001)}`,
        base_uri: `ipfs://${CID('e')}/`,
        payout_address: payer,
        royalty_bps: 500,
        royalty_receiver: payer,
        public_max_per_wallet: 3,
        mint_fee_flat_wei: 0,
        mint_fee_bps: 0,
        airdrop_fee_per_token_wei: 0,
      })
      .select()
    return expectError(error as DbError, PERMISSION, data)
  })

  await check(
    "collection_record_deploy: the owner's session and a signed-out caller cannot execute it",
    async () => {
      const problems: string[] = []
      for (const [who, client] of [
        ['owner session', A.client],
        ['signed out', anon],
      ] as const) {
        const { error } = await callArt(
          client,
          'collection_record_deploy',
          recordArgs(A.userId, rendered.cid, payer, 1),
        )
        const problem = expectSentence(error, PERMISSION, 'permission denied')
        if (problem) problems.push(`${who}: ${problem}`)
      }
      return problems.length ? problems.join('; ') : null
    },
  )

  await check("collection_record_deploy: B's id as p_owner on A's collection is refused as not found", async () => {
    const { error } = await callArt(
      admin,
      'collection_record_deploy',
      recordArgs(B.userId, rendered.cid, payer, 1),
    )
    const problem = expectSentence(error, PERMISSION, 'collection not found')
    if (problem) return problem
    return (await deployedState(rendered.cid)).status === 'rendered' ? null : 'the collection changed'
  })

  await check(
    'collection_record_deploy: a collection without a live link is refused, lapsed or missing',
    async () => {
      const missing = await callArt(
        admin,
        'collection_record_deploy',
        recordArgs(A.userId, unlinked.cid, payer, 2),
      )
      const first = expectSentence(missing.error, CHECK, 'Reserve your mint page link first.')
      if (first) return `no link: ${first}`
      const reserved = await reserve(A.client, unlinked.cid, slug())
      if (reserved) return `setup reserve: ${describe(reserved)}`
      const aged = await admin
        .from('collections')
        .update({ slug_reserved_at: new Date(Date.now() - 4 * 24 * 3600 * 1000).toISOString() })
        .eq('id', unlinked.cid)
      if (aged.error) return `setup age: ${describe(aged.error as DbError)}`
      const lapsed = await callArt(
        admin,
        'collection_record_deploy',
        recordArgs(A.userId, unlinked.cid, payer, 2),
      )
      const second = expectSentence(lapsed.error, CHECK, 'Reserve your mint page link first.')
      if (second) return `lapsed link: ${second}`
      return (await deployedState(unlinked.cid)).status === 'rendered' ? null : 'the collection changed'
    },
  )

  await check('collection_record_deploy: a deferred collection without a supply is refused', async () => {
    const { error } = await callArt(admin, 'collection_record_deploy', recordArgs(A.userId, bare.cid, payer, 3))
    return expectSentence(error, CHECK, 'Set the supply in Art first.')
  })

  await check('collection_record_deploy: a collection with nothing rendered is refused', async () => {
    const supply = await A.client.from('collections').update({ max_supply: 3 }).eq('id', draftId)
    if (supply.error) return `setup supply: ${describe(supply.error as DbError)}`
    const link = await reserve(A.client, draftId, slug())
    if (link) return `setup link: ${describe(link)}`
    const { error } = await callArt(admin, 'collection_record_deploy', recordArgs(A.userId, draftId, payer, 4))
    return expectSentence(error, CHECK, 'Render the art before deploying.')
  })

  let deployedSlug: string | null = null
  await check(
    'collection_record_deploy: records the deployment, marks the collection deployed on its chain, makes the link permanent and writes the launch phases',
    async () => {
      const stale = await A.client
        .from('mint_groups')
        .insert({ collection_id: rendered.cid, onchain_id: 7, name: 'Old draft' })
      if (stale.error) return `setup group: ${describe(stale.error as DbError)}`
      const before = await deployedState(rendered.cid)
      deployedSlug = before.slug
      const { data, error } = await callArt(
        admin,
        'collection_record_deploy',
        recordArgs(A.userId, rendered.cid, payer, 1, { p_mint_fee_flat_wei: BIG_FEE }),
      )
      if (error) return describe(error)
      const returned = data as { collection_id?: string; tx_hash?: string } | null
      if (returned?.collection_id !== rendered.cid || returned.tx_hash !== `0x${HEX64(2001)}`)
        return `returned ${JSON.stringify(returned)}`
      const state = await deployedState(rendered.cid)
      const problems = [
        state.status === 'deployed' ? null : `status ${state.status}`,
        state.chain_id === 31337 ? null : `chain ${state.chain_id}`,
        state.contract_address === ADDRESS40(1) ? null : `contract ${state.contract_address}`,
        state.slug === before.slug ? null : `slug ${state.slug}`,
        state.slug_reserved_at === null ? null : `slug_reserved_at ${state.slug_reserved_at}`,
      ].filter(Boolean)
      if (problems.length) return problems.join('; ')
      const groups = await groupsOf(rendered.cid)
      const want = [
        { onchain_id: 0, name: 'Public', price_wei: '0', max_supply: 2, start_at: null },
        { onchain_id: 1, name: 'Holders', price_wei: '1000000000000000', max_supply: 1, start_at: null },
      ]
      if (JSON.stringify(groups) !== JSON.stringify(want)) return `groups ${JSON.stringify(groups)}`
      const { row, error: readError } = await deploymentOf(A.client, rendered.cid)
      if (readError) return `read: ${describe(readError)}`
      if (!row) return 'no deployments row under the owner'
      const age = Date.now() - Date.parse(row.deployed_at)
      return row.chain_id === 31337 &&
        row.factory === FACTORY_ADDRESS &&
        row.deployer === payer &&
        row.salt === `0x${HEX64(1)}` &&
        row.code_hash === `0x${HEX64(1001)}` &&
        row.contract_address === ADDRESS40(1) &&
        row.base_uri === `ipfs://${CID('e')}/` &&
        row.royalty_bps === 500 &&
        row.public_max_per_wallet === 3 &&
        Math.abs(age) < 60_000
        ? null
        : JSON.stringify(row)
    },
  )

  await check('collection_record_deploy: the fee snapshot keeps every wei above 2^53', async () => {
    const { row, error } = await deploymentOf(A.client, rendered.cid)
    if (error) return describe(error)
    return row?.mint_fee_flat_wei === BIG_FEE ? null : `mint_fee_flat_wei ${row?.mint_fee_flat_wei}`
  })

  await check('collection_record_deploy: a second call for a deployed collection is refused', async () => {
    const { error } = await callArt(
      admin,
      'collection_record_deploy',
      recordArgs(A.userId, rendered.cid, payer, 5),
    )
    const problem = expectSentence(error, CHECK, 'The contract is already deployed.')
    if (problem) return problem
    const { row } = await deploymentOf(A.client, rendered.cid)
    return row?.tx_hash === `0x${HEX64(2001)}` ? null : `the row changed: ${row?.tx_hash}`
  })

  await check(
    'collection_record_deploy: a transaction hash recorded for another collection is refused',
    async () => {
      const { error } = await callArt(
        admin,
        'collection_record_deploy',
        recordArgs(A.userId, deferred.cid, payer, 6, { p_tx_hash: `0x${HEX64(2001)}` }),
      )
      const problem = expectSentence(error, CHECK, 'This transaction was recorded for another collection.')
      if (problem) return problem
      return (await deployedState(deferred.cid)).status === 'draft' ? null : 'the collection changed'
    },
  )

  await check('collection_record_deploy: a deferred collection deploys on its placeholder base URI', async () => {
    const { error } = await callArt(
      admin,
      'collection_record_deploy',
      recordArgs(A.userId, deferred.cid, payer, 6, { p_base_uri: `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json` }),
    )
    if (error) return describe(error)
    const state = await deployedState(deferred.cid)
    const { row } = await deploymentOf(A.client, deferred.cid)
    return state.status === 'deployed' &&
      state.chain_id === 31337 &&
      state.art_locked_at === null &&
      row?.base_uri === `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json`
      ? null
      : `${JSON.stringify(state)} ${JSON.stringify(row)}`
  })

  await check(
    "reserve_slug: a deployed collection's link is permanent, so another collection cannot take it",
    async () => {
      if (!deployedSlug) return 'no slug recorded'
      const other = await insertCollection(B.client)
      if (!other.row) return `setup: ${describe(other.error)}`
      const problem = expectError(await reserve(B.client, other.row.id, deployedSlug), UNIQUE)
      if (problem) return problem
      return (await deployedState(rendered.cid)).slug === deployedSlug ? null : 'the deployed link was lost'
    },
  )

  await check(
    "deployments: B cannot read A's deployment; A reads it and cannot update or delete it",
    async () => {
      const strangers = await B.client
        .from('deployments')
        .select('collection_id')
        .eq('collection_id', rendered.cid)
      const hidden = expectNothing(strangers.error as DbError, strangers.data)
      if (hidden) return `B: ${hidden}`
      const own = await deploymentOf(A.client, rendered.cid)
      if (own.error || !own.row) return `A: ${describe(own.error)}`
      const update = await A.client
        .from('deployments')
        .update({ royalty_bps: 1 })
        .eq('collection_id', rendered.cid)
        .select()
      const refused = expectError(update.error as DbError, PERMISSION, update.data)
      if (refused) return `update: ${refused}`
      const del = await A.client.from('deployments').delete().eq('collection_id', rendered.cid).select()
      const kept = expectNothing(del.error as DbError, del.data)
      if (kept) return `delete: ${kept}`
      return (await deploymentOf(A.client, rendered.cid)).row?.royalty_bps === 500 ? null : 'the row changed'
    },
  )

  await check('collection_placeholder_ready: true with a done placeholder job, false without', async () => {
    const ready = await A.client.rpc('collection_placeholder_ready', { p_collection_id: deferred.cid })
    if (ready.error) return describe(ready.error as DbError)
    const not = await A.client.rpc('collection_placeholder_ready', { p_collection_id: rendered.cid })
    if (not.error) return describe(not.error as DbError)
    return ready.data === true && not.data === false
      ? null
      : JSON.stringify({ ready: ready.data, not: not.data })
  })

  await check(
    "collection_placeholder_ready: B's session on A's collection is refused as not found; anon and the service role cannot execute it",
    async () => {
      const stranger = await B.client.rpc('collection_placeholder_ready', { p_collection_id: deferred.cid })
      const notFound = expectSentence(stranger.error as DbError, PERMISSION, 'collection not found')
      if (notFound) return `B: ${notFound}`
      const problems: string[] = []
      for (const [who, client] of [
        ['signed out', anon],
        ['service role', admin],
      ] as const) {
        const { error } = await client.rpc('collection_placeholder_ready', { p_collection_id: deferred.cid })
        const problem = expectSentence(error as DbError, PERMISSION, 'permission denied')
        if (problem) problems.push(`${who}: ${problem}`)
      }
      return problems.length ? problems.join('; ') : null
    },
  )

  await check(
    'collections_guard_frozen_fields: the name, symbol and description cannot change while the art is locked',
    async () => {
      const before = await stored(locked.cid)
      if (!before?.art_locked_at) return 'setup: the collection is not locked'
      const problems: string[] = []
      for (const patch of [{ name: 'Renamed' }, { symbol: 'NEW' }, { description: 'Changed' }]) {
        const { data, error } = await A.client.from('collections').update(patch).eq('id', locked.cid).select()
        const problem =
          expectError(error as DbError, CHECK, data) ?? expectSentence(error as DbError, CHECK, LOCK_SENTENCE)
        if (problem) problems.push(`${Object.keys(patch)[0]}: ${problem}`)
      }
      if (problems.length) return problems.join('; ')
      return sameStoredRow(before, await stored(locked.cid)) ? null : 'stored row changed'
    },
  )

  await check(
    'collections_guard_frozen_fields: the three still change on a draft, and again after an unlock',
    async () => {
      const changed = await A.client
        .from('collections')
        .update({ name: 'Draft renamed', symbol: 'DRF', description: 'A draft can change.' })
        .eq('id', draftId)
        .select()
      if (changed.error) return `draft: ${describe(changed.error as DbError)}`
      const unlock = await callArt(A.client, 'art_unlock', { p_collection_id: locked.cid })
      if (unlock.error) return `unlock: ${describe(unlock.error)}`
      const after = await A.client
        .from('collections')
        .update({ name: 'Unlocked renamed', description: 'Open again.' })
        .eq('id', locked.cid)
        .select()
      if (after.error) return `after unlock: ${describe(after.error as DbError)}`
      return (await stored(locked.cid))?.name === 'Unlocked renamed' ? null : 'the rename was not stored'
    },
  )

  await check(
    'collections_guard_frozen_fields: a deployed collection whose placeholder alone was locked keeps the deployment branch',
    async () => {
      const name = await A.client.from('collections').update({ name: 'Too late' }).eq('id', deferred.cid).select()
      const refused =
        expectError(name.error as DbError, CHECK, name.data) ??
        expectSentence(name.error as DbError, CHECK, 'The name and symbol cannot change after deployment.')
      if (refused) return `name: ${refused}`
      const description = await A.client
        .from('collections')
        .update({ description: 'The description still changes.' })
        .eq('id', deferred.cid)
        .select()
      if (description.error) return `description: ${describe(description.error as DbError)}`
      return description.data.length === 1 ? null : 'the description did not change'
    },
  )
}
```

### Task 4: The deploy sentences, the pure argument helpers, the event matcher, the cores, the write helper and the actions

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/web/src/lib/art/result.ts` (append the deploy sentences)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/lib/art/db-error.ts` (imports; `deployWriteError`)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/lib/art/lock-collection.ts` (three columns)
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-types.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-args.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-args.test.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/chain/collection-deployed.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/chain/collection-deployed.test.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-write.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/actions/deploy.ts`
- Test: `deploy-args.test.ts`, `collection-deployed.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `collectionSalt`, `encodeCollectionArgs`, `predictCollectionAddress`, `CollectionArgs`, `factoryAbi`, `chainById`, `availableChains`, `factoryAddress`, `confirmationsFor`, `hasConfirmations` from `@cobweb/shared` (Tasks 1 and 2); `COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE`, `COLLECTION_CODE_HASH` from `@cobweb/shared/collection` (Task 1); `publicClientFor` from `@cobweb/shared/server`; `LockContext`, `LockOutcome`, `loadJob`, `errorSummary` from `@/lib/art/lock-core`; `JOB_COLUMNS`, `JobRow` from `@/lib/art/render-job`; `ReceiptLike` from `@/lib/chain/art-paid`; `slugState` from `@/lib/collections`; `chainView` from `@/lib/chain/chain-view`; `parse` from `@/lib/art/schemas`; `txHash` from `@/lib/art/lock-schemas`; `ownedCollection`, `createServiceClient`; the `collection_record_deploy` and `deployments` types (Task 3).
- Produces:
  - sentences in `@/lib/art/result`: `ALREADY_DEPLOYED`, `LINK_REQUIRED`, `SUPPLY_REQUIRED`, `NOT_RENDERED`, `DEPLOY_USED`, `WRONG_CHAIN`, `FACTORY_UNREADABLE`, `DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED`, `DEPLOY_REVERTED`, `NOT_THIS_DEPLOY`, `OTHER_CODE`, `OTHER_CONTRACT`, `DEPLOY_TIMED_OUT`
  - `deployWriteError(error: DbError): string` in `@/lib/art/db-error`
  - types in `@/lib/deploy/deploy-types`: `DeployGroup`, `DeployForm`, `PreparedArgs`, `Prepared`, `DeployRecordGroup`, `DeployRecord`, `DoneJob`, `DeployJobs`, `DeployView`, `DeployChain`

2. At the end of the file (after `lockWriteError`), append:

```ts

/** Sentences collection_record_deploy raises (supabase/migrations/20261014100000_deploy.sql). */
const DEPLOY_SENTENCES = new Set([ALREADY_DEPLOYED, LINK_REQUIRED, SUPPLY_REQUIRED, NOT_RENDERED, DEPLOY_USED])

/** A failed deploy write, by SQLSTATE, in the creator's words (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 6). */
export function deployWriteError(error: DbError): string {
  if (!error) return UNSAVED
  if (error.code === '42501') return error.message === 'collection not found' ? NOT_FOUND : UNSAVED
  // Two unique indexes: one deployment per transaction, one per collection (the primary key).
  if (error.code === '23505')
    return error.message.includes('deployments_tx_hash_key') ? DEPLOY_USED : ALREADY_DEPLOYED
  if (error.code === '23514' && DEPLOY_SENTENCES.has(error.message)) return error.message
  return UNSAVED
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The types**

Create `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-types.ts`:

```ts
import type { Address, Hex } from 'viem'
import type { FeeSnapshot, RenderJob, SupportedChain } from '@/lib/demo/types'

/** One launch phase as the creator fills it in: index 0 is the public sale. */
export type DeployGroup = { name: string; priceWei: string; maxSupply: number }

/** What the deploy screen sends to prepare and to confirm (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 6.1). */
export type DeployForm = {
  /** The picked chain; must equal the done real job's quote chain when there is one. */
  chainId: number
  payout: Address
  royaltyBps: number
  royaltyReceiver: Address
  publicMaxPerWallet: number
  /** Index 0 the public sale, then up to nine more. */
  groups: DeployGroup[]
}

/** The constructor arguments as prepared and as stored in `deployments.args`: every uint a decimal string. */
export type PreparedArgs = {
  name: string
  symbol: string
  payout: Address
  baseUri: string
  maxSupply: string
  groupSupplies: string[]
  groupPrices: string[]
  publicMaxPerWallet: string
  royaltyBps: number
  owner: Address
  royaltyReceiver: Address
}

/** What `prepareDeploy` answers: everything the browser sends, and what the server proves again at confirm. */
export type Prepared = {
  chainId: number
  factory: Address
  deployer: Address
  salt: Hex
  codeHash: Hex
  args: PreparedArgs
  /** The validated launch phases, names trimmed, in group order. */
  groups: DeployGroup[]
  encodedArgs: Hex
  predicted: Address
  /** The base URI the contract starts on. */
  baseUri: string
  /** The real set's base URI when the contract starts on a placeholder and the real set is rendered; else null. */
  revealsTo: string | null
  fees: FeeSnapshot
}

export type DeployRecordGroup = { onchainId: number; name: string; priceWei: string; maxSupply: number }

/** A deployed collection as `deployments` and `mint_groups` record it, read under the creator's client. */
export type DeployRecord = {
  chainId: number
  contractAddress: Address
  txHash: Hex
  deployer: Address
  /** Unix seconds. */
  deployedAt: number
  baseUri: string
  payout: Address
  royaltyBps: number
  royaltyReceiver: Address
  publicMaxPerWallet: number
  fees: FeeSnapshot
  groups: DeployRecordGroup[]
}

export type DoneJob = Extract<RenderJob, { state: 'done' }>

/** The finished jobs the deploy starts from: `start` is the one the contract points at. */
export type DeployJobs = { start: DoneJob | null; real: DoneJob | null }

/** What the deploy page needs from the server in one read. */
export type DeployView = {
  jobs: DeployJobs
  /** The chain the fee was paid on (the done real job's quote chain), or null when the creator picks. */
  chainId: number | null
  /** The factory's fee snapshot on that chain, read on the server; null when unreadable or when there is no chain yet. */
  fees: FeeSnapshot | null
  record: DeployRecord | null
}

/** A chain the deploy screen offers, with its factory when the environment names one. */
export type DeployChain = SupportedChain & { factory: Address | null }
```

```ts
const RECEIVER = '0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC'

    { name: 'Holders', priceWei: '3000000000000000', maxSupply: 177 },

}

    ['the zero address as payout', { payout: ZERO }, PAYOUT_ZERO],
    ['a receiver that is not an address', { royaltyReceiver: '0x1234' }, RECEIVER_INVALID],
    ['the zero address as receiver', { royaltyReceiver: ZERO }, RECEIVER_ZERO],
    ['a royalty above 1,000 bps', { royaltyBps: 1001 }, ROYALTY_RANGE],
    ['a royalty that is not whole', { royaltyBps: 2.5 }, ROYALTY_RANGE],
    ['a public limit of 0', { publicMaxPerWallet: 0 }, CAP_RANGE],
    ['a public limit above the supply', { publicMaxPerWallet: 778 }, CAP_RANGE],
    ['no phases', { groups: [] }, GROUP_COUNT],
    [
      'eleven phases',
      { groups: Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => ({ name: `Phase ${i}`, priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 1 })) },
      GROUP_COUNT,
    ],
    ['an empty phase name', { groups: [{ name: '  ', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 1 }] }, GROUP_NAME],
    ['a phase name over 40 characters', { groups: [{ name: 'x'.repeat(41), priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 1 }] }, GROUP_NAME],
    [
      'two phases with the same name, whatever the case',
      {

    ],

  })

})

describe('resolveDeployChain (Review Focus 2)', () => {

    expect(resolveDeployChain(8453, 1)).toBeNull()

  const revealLater = { id: 'real', placeholder_upload_id: 'upload-1' }
  const placeholder = { id: 'placeholder', placeholder_upload_id: 'upload-1' }

    expect(startJobOf({ artDeferred: false, real: revealLater, placeholder: null })).toBe(revealLater)
  })

    expect(startJobOf({ artDeferred: false, real, placeholder })).toBe(real)
    expect(startJobOf({ artDeferred: false, real: null, placeholder: null })).toBeNull()
  })
})

    expect(revealsToOf({ id: 'real' }, real)).toBeNull()
    expect(revealsToOf({ id: 'placeholder' }, null)).toBeNull()
    expect(revealsToOf(null, real)).toBeNull()

})
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/chain/collection-deployed.test.ts`:

```ts
import { factoryAbi } from '@cobweb/shared'
import { encodeAbiParameters, encodeEventTopics, type Address, type Hex } from 'viem'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { NOT_THIS_DEPLOY, OTHER_CODE, OTHER_CONTRACT } from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { ReceiptLike } from './art-paid'
import { matchCollectionDeployed } from './collection-deployed'

const FACTORY: Address = '0x5FbDB2315678afecb367f032d93F642f64180aa3'
const ELSEWHERE: Address = '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000AbC'
const DEPLOYER: Address = '0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8'
const STRANGER: Address = '0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC'
const PREDICTED: Address = '0x0409454C375c8a6aB05383bFB6D9FcDe9ebB8F85'
const OTHER: Address = '0x2073D2716161E2Fa4eBcB3BA42348dE8f98A3dDd'
const SALT: Hex = `0x0f4a0f3e2f5e4e3a9d1c1b2c3d4e5f60${'0'.repeat(32)}`
const OTHER_SALT: Hex = `0x${'1'.repeat(64)}`
const CODE_HASH: Hex = '0xa57eac3d6d8dca5d3bb294162c964342bd351cba3cf034f1b8b6b3e2ca9ae594'
const OTHER_HASH: Hex = `0x${'b'.repeat(64)}`

type ReceiptLog = ReceiptLike['logs'][number]

function deployed(
  address: Address,
  args: { collection: Address; codeHash: Hex; deployer: Address; salt: Hex },
): ReceiptLog {
  const topics = encodeEventTopics({
    abi: factoryAbi,
    eventName: 'CollectionDeployed',
    args: { collection: args.collection, codeHash: args.codeHash, deployer: args.deployer },
  })
  return {
    address,
    topics: topics as ReceiptLog['topics'],
    data: encodeAbiParameters([{ type: 'bytes32' }], [args.salt]),
  }
}

const receipt = (...logs: ReceiptLog[]): ReceiptLike => ({ status: 'success', logs })
const expected = { factory: FACTORY, deployer: DEPLOYER, salt: SALT, codeHash: CODE_HASH, predicted: PREDICTED }
const good = { collection: PREDICTED, codeHash: CODE_HASH, deployer: DEPLOYER, salt: SALT }

describe('matchCollectionDeployed', () => {
  it("matches the factory's CollectionDeployed log for this deployer and salt at the predicted address", () => {
    expect(matchCollectionDeployed(receipt(deployed(FACTORY, good)), expected)).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      collection: PREDICTED,
    })
    // The address is answered checksummed whatever the log's case.
    expect(
      matchCollectionDeployed(
        receipt(deployed(FACTORY, { ...good, collection: PREDICTED.toLowerCase() as Address })),
        { ...expected, predicted: PREDICTED.toLowerCase() as Address },
      ),
    ).toEqual({ ok: true, collection: PREDICTED })
  })

  it('refuses a receipt with no CollectionDeployed log, and one that reverted', () => {
    expect(matchCollectionDeployed(receipt(), expected)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_DEPLOY })
    expect(matchCollectionDeployed({ status: 'reverted', logs: [deployed(FACTORY, good)] }, expected)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: NOT_THIS_DEPLOY,
    })
  })

  it('ignores a CollectionDeployed log emitted by any other contract (Review Focus 1)', () => {
    expect(matchCollectionDeployed(receipt(deployed(ELSEWHERE, good)), expected)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: NOT_THIS_DEPLOY,
    })
  })

  it('refuses a log for another deployer or another salt (Review Focus 1)', () => {
    expect(matchCollectionDeployed(receipt(deployed(FACTORY, { ...good, deployer: STRANGER })), expected)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: NOT_THIS_DEPLOY,
    })
    expect(matchCollectionDeployed(receipt(deployed(FACTORY, { ...good, salt: OTHER_SALT })), expected)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: NOT_THIS_DEPLOY,
    })
  })

  it('refuses a deployment of other contract code', () => {
    expect(matchCollectionDeployed(receipt(deployed(FACTORY, { ...good, codeHash: OTHER_HASH })), expected)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: OTHER_CODE,
    })
  })

  it('refuses a collection built from other arguments: the address differs from the prediction (Review Focus 1)', () => {
    expect(matchCollectionDeployed(receipt(deployed(FACTORY, { ...good, collection: OTHER })), expected)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: OTHER_CONTRACT,
    })
  })

  it("skips the factory's other logs and finds the matching one among several", () => {
    const unrelated: ReceiptLog = { address: FACTORY, topics: [`0x${'12'.repeat(32)}`], data: '0x' }
    expect(
      matchCollectionDeployed(
        receipt(
          unrelated,
          deployed(FACTORY, { ...good, salt: OTHER_SALT, collection: OTHER }),
          deployed(FACTORY, { ...good, codeHash: OTHER_HASH }),
          deployed(FACTORY, good),
        ),
        expected,
      ),
    ).toEqual({ ok: true, collection: PREDICTED })
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The pure helpers**

Create `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-args.ts`:

```ts
import type { CollectionArgs } from '@cobweb/shared'
import { getAddress, isAddress, type Address } from 'viem'
import { z } from 'zod'
import { parse } from '@/lib/art/schemas'
import type { DeployForm, PreparedArgs } from './deploy-types'

/**
 * The deploy form's rules and the constructor built from it (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md,
 * section 6.1). Pure, so the screen, the cores and the tests share them.
 */
export const MAX_LAUNCH_GROUPS = 10
export const GROUP_NAME_MAX = 40
export const MAX_ROYALTY_BPS = 1000

export const CHAIN_REQUIRED = 'Choose a chain to deploy on.'
export const PAYOUT_INVALID = 'Enter a valid payout address.'
export const PAYOUT_ZERO = 'The zero address cannot receive proceeds.'
export const RECEIVER_INVALID = 'Enter a valid royalty receiver.'
export const RECEIVER_ZERO = 'The zero address cannot receive royalties.'
export const ROYALTY_RANGE = 'The royalty is 0 to 1,000 basis points.'
export const CAP_RANGE = 'The public mint limit is a whole number from 1 to the supply.'
export const GROUP_COUNT = 'The contract starts with 1 to 10 phases.'
export const GROUP_NAME = 'A phase name has 1 to 40 characters.'
export const GROUP_NAMES_UNIQUE = 'Two phases cannot have the same name.'
export const GROUP_PRICE = 'A phase price is a whole number of wei.'
export const GROUP_SUPPLY = 'A phase allocation is a whole number of tokens.'
export const GROUPS_OVER_SUPPLY = 'The phases hold more tokens than the supply.'

const ZERO_ADDRESS = /^0x0{40}$/i
const address = (invalid: string, zero: string) =>
  z
    .string()
    .refine((value) => isAddress(value), invalid)
    .refine((value) => !ZERO_ADDRESS.test(value), zero)

/** The form's shape, before the bounds the supply sets (`validateDeployForm`). */
export const deployFormShape = z.object({
  chainId: z.number().int(CHAIN_REQUIRED).positive(CHAIN_REQUIRED),
  payout: address(PAYOUT_INVALID, PAYOUT_ZERO),
  royaltyBps: z.number().int(ROYALTY_RANGE).min(0, ROYALTY_RANGE).max(MAX_ROYALTY_BPS, ROYALTY_RANGE),
  royaltyReceiver: address(RECEIVER_INVALID, RECEIVER_ZERO),
  publicMaxPerWallet: z.number().int(CAP_RANGE).min(1, CAP_RANGE),
  groups: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        name: z.string().trim().min(1, GROUP_NAME).max(GROUP_NAME_MAX, GROUP_NAME),
        priceWei: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, GROUP_PRICE),
        maxSupply: z.number().int(GROUP_SUPPLY).min(0, GROUP_SUPPLY),
      }),
    )
    .min(1, GROUP_COUNT)
    .max(MAX_LAUNCH_GROUPS, GROUP_COUNT)
    .refine(
      (groups) => new Set(groups.map((group) => group.name.toLowerCase())).size === groups.length,
      GROUP_NAMES_UNIQUE,
    ),
})

/** The form as the core accepts it: the shape, then the bounds the supply sets. Addresses come back checksummed. */
export function validateDeployForm(
  input: unknown,
  maxSupply: number,
): { ok: true; data: DeployForm } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  const shape = parse(deployFormShape, input)
  if (!shape.ok) return shape
  const form = shape.data
  if (form.publicMaxPerWallet > maxSupply) return { ok: false, error: CAP_RANGE }
  const allocated = form.groups.reduce((sum, group) => sum + group.maxSupply, 0)
  if (allocated > maxSupply) return { ok: false, error: GROUPS_OVER_SUPPLY }
  return {
    ok: true,
    data: {
      chainId: form.chainId,
      payout: getAddress(form.payout),
      royaltyBps: form.royaltyBps,
      royaltyReceiver: getAddress(form.royaltyReceiver),
      publicMaxPerWallet: form.publicMaxPerWallet,
      groups: form.groups.map((group) => ({
        name: group.name,
        priceWei: group.priceWei,
        maxSupply: group.maxSupply,
      })),
    },
  }
}

/**
 * The chain the contract goes on (spec decision 2): the chain the fee was paid on when a done real
 * job set one (the form must agree), else the form's. Null on a mismatch.
 */
export function resolveDeployChain(paidChainId: number | null, formChainId: number): number | null {
  if (paidChainId !== null && paidChainId !== formChainId) return null
  return paidChainId ?? formChainId
}

/**
 * Which done job the contract starts on (section 6.1): the placeholder job when the collection
 * finishes its art later or the real job carries "Reveal later" (`placeholder_upload_id` set), else
 * the real job; null when there is nothing to start on.
 */
export function startJobOf<T extends { placeholder_upload_id: string | null }>(input: {
  artDeferred: boolean
  real: T | null
  placeholder: T | null
}): T | null {
  const wantsPlaceholder =
    input.artDeferred || (input.real !== null && input.real.placeholder_upload_id !== null)
  if (wantsPlaceholder && input.placeholder) return input.placeholder
  return input.real
}

/** The real set's base URI, when the contract starts on another job and the real set is rendered. */
export function revealsToOf(
  start: { id: string } | null,
  real: { id: string; base_uri: string | null } | null,
): string | null {
  if (!start || !real || real.id === start.id) return null
  return real.base_uri
}

/**
 * The constructor arguments: the collection row's name, symbol and supply, the start job's base URI,
 * the validated form, and the session wallet as the owner (the deployer).
 */
export function deployArgs(
  collection: { name: string; symbol: string; max_supply: number },
  job: { base_uri: string },
  form: DeployForm,
  wallet: Address,
): PreparedArgs {
  return {
    name: collection.name,
    symbol: collection.symbol,
    payout: form.payout,
    baseUri: job.base_uri,
    maxSupply: String(collection.max_supply),
    groupSupplies: form.groups.map((group) => String(group.maxSupply)),
    groupPrices: form.groups.map((group) => group.priceWei),
    publicMaxPerWallet: String(form.publicMaxPerWallet),
    royaltyBps: form.royaltyBps,
    owner: wallet,
    royaltyReceiver: form.royaltyReceiver,
  }
}

/** `PreparedArgs` as `encodeCollectionArgs` takes them. */
export function collectionArgsOf(args: PreparedArgs): CollectionArgs {
  return {
    name: args.name,
    symbol: args.symbol,
    payout: args.payout,
    baseUri: args.baseUri,
    maxSupply: BigInt(args.maxSupply),
    groupSupplies: args.groupSupplies.map((value) => BigInt(value)),
    groupPrices: args.groupPrices.map((value) => BigInt(value)),
    publicMaxPerWallet: BigInt(args.publicMaxPerWallet),
    royaltyBps: args.royaltyBps,
    owner: args.owner,
    royaltyReceiver: args.royaltyReceiver,
  }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/chain/collection-deployed.ts`:

```ts
import { factoryAbi } from '@cobweb/shared'
import { decodeEventLog, getAddress, type Address, type Hex } from 'viem'
import { NOT_THIS_DEPLOY, OTHER_CODE, OTHER_CONTRACT } from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { ReceiptLike } from './art-paid'

export type CollectionDeployedMatch = { ok: true; collection: Address } | { ok: false; error: string }

/**
 * Finds the factory's `CollectionDeployed` log for this deployer and salt in a receipt and checks it
 * against what the server prepared (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 6.2): the log must come
 * from the factory's address and name the deployer and the salt (`NOT_THIS_DEPLOY` otherwise), carry
 * the shipped code hash (`OTHER_CODE`), and its address must equal the prediction (`OTHER_CONTRACT`:
 * the arguments on chain are not the ones prepared). The address comes from the log, never from the browser.
 */
export function matchCollectionDeployed(
  receipt: ReceiptLike,
  expected: { factory: Address; deployer: Address; salt: Hex; codeHash: Hex; predicted: Address },
): CollectionDeployedMatch {
  if (receipt.status !== 'success') return { ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_DEPLOY }
  const deployed: { collection: Address; codeHash: Hex }[] = []
  for (const log of receipt.logs) {
    if (log.address.toLowerCase() !== expected.factory.toLowerCase()) continue
    try {
      const event = decodeEventLog({
        abi: factoryAbi,
        eventName: 'CollectionDeployed',
        data: log.data,
        topics: log.topics,
      })
      if (
        event.args.deployer.toLowerCase() === expected.deployer.toLowerCase() &&
        event.args.salt.toLowerCase() === expected.salt.toLowerCase()
      )
        deployed.push({ collection: event.args.collection, codeHash: event.args.codeHash })
    } catch {
      // Another event of the factory.
    }
  }
  if (deployed.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: NOT_THIS_DEPLOY }
  const sameCode = deployed.filter((entry) => entry.codeHash.toLowerCase() === expected.codeHash.toLowerCase())
  if (sameCode.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: OTHER_CODE }
  const match = sameCode.find((entry) => entry.collection.toLowerCase() === expected.predicted.toLowerCase())
  if (!match) return { ok: false, error: OTHER_CONTRACT }
  return { ok: true, collection: getAddress(match.collection) }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts`:

```ts
import {
  availableChains,
  chainById,
  collectionSalt,
  confirmationsFor,
  encodeCollectionArgs,
  factoryAbi,
  factoryAddress,
  hasConfirmations,
  predictCollectionAddress,
} from '@cobweb/shared'
import { COLLECTION_CODE_HASH, COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE } from '@cobweb/shared/collection'
import { publicClientFor } from '@cobweb/shared/server'
import { getAddress, TransactionReceiptNotFoundError, type Address, type Hex, type PublicClient } from 'viem'
import type { LockClient } from '@/lib/art/lock-collection'
import { errorSummary, loadJob, type LockContext, type LockOutcome } from '@/lib/art/lock-core'
import { JOB_COLUMNS, type JobRow } from '@/lib/art/render-job'
import {
  ALREADY_DEPLOYED,
  CHAIN_NOT_READY,
  DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED,
  DEPLOY_REVERTED,
  DEPLOY_USED,
  FACTORY_UNREADABLE,
  LINK_REQUIRED,
  NOT_RENDERED,
  SUPPLY_REQUIRED,
  WRONG_CHAIN,
} from '@/lib/art/result'
import { chainView } from '@/lib/chain/chain-view'
import { matchCollectionDeployed } from '@/lib/chain/collection-deployed'
import { slugState } from '@/lib/collections'
import type { Json } from '@/lib/database.types'
import type { FeeSnapshot } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import {
  collectionArgsOf,
  deployArgs,
  resolveDeployChain,
  revealsToOf,
  startJobOf,
  validateDeployForm,
} from './deploy-args'
import type { DeployChain, DeployForm, DeployRecord, DeployView, DoneJob, Prepared } from './deploy-types'

/**
 * The deploy steps (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 6) on a Supabase client acting as the
 * signed-in creator. The server actions (app/actions/deploy.ts) call these after the session and
 * ownership check; verify:deploy calls them with a signed-in script client. No `server-only` marker,
 * so the script can import it; the browser never does.
 */
export type DeployContext = Omit<LockContext, 'queue'>
export type DeployOutcome<T> = LockOutcome<T>

/** The factory's `collectionFees()` on a chain as the screens show it, or null when the chain, its RPC or its factory is not there. */
export async function readCollectionFees(chainId: number): Promise<FeeSnapshot | null> {
  try {
    const [flat, bps, airdrop] = await publicClientFor(chainId).readContract({
      address: factoryAddress(chainId),
      abi: factoryAbi,
      functionName: 'collectionFees',
    })
    return { mintFeeFlatWei: flat.toString(), mintFeeBps: Number(bps), airdropFeePerTokenWei: airdrop.toString() }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`The collection fees could not be read on chain ${chainId}: ${errorSummary(error)}`)
    return null
  }
}

function factoryOn(chainId: number): Address | null {
  try {
    return factoryAddress(chainId)
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

/** The chains the deploy screen offers (the launch chains, plus Sepolia and Anvil outside production), each with its factory when configured. */
export function deployChains(): DeployChain[] {
  return availableChains().map((chain) => ({ ...chainView(chain.id), factory: factoryOn(chain.id) }))
}

/** The collection's latest done job of a kind, or null. */
async function doneJobRow(
  supabase: LockClient,
  collectionId: string,
  kind: 'real' | 'placeholder',
): Promise<JobRow | null> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('render_jobs')
    .select(JOB_COLUMNS)
    .eq('collection_id', collectionId)
    .eq('kind', kind)
    .eq('state', 'done')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(1)
    .maybeSingle()
  if (error) throw new Error(`The render job could not be read: ${error.message}`)
  return data as unknown as JobRow | null
}

type DoneRows = { start: JobRow | null; real: JobRow | null }

async function doneRows(ctx: DeployContext): Promise<DoneRows> {
  const [real, placeholder] = await Promise.all([
    doneJobRow(ctx.supabase, ctx.collection.id, 'real'),
    doneJobRow(ctx.supabase, ctx.collection.id, 'placeholder'),
  ])
  return { start: startJobOf({ artDeferred: ctx.collection.art_deferred, real, placeholder }), real }
}

async function asDoneJob(supabase: LockClient, row: JobRow | null): Promise<DoneJob | null> {
  if (!row) return null
  const job = await loadJob(supabase, row.id)
  if (job.state !== 'done') throw new Error(`Render job ${row.id} is not done.`)
  return job
}

/** The refusals every deploy step shares, in the order the creator should read them (section 6.1). */
function refusalBefore(ctx: DeployContext, rows: DoneRows): string | null {
  const { collection } = ctx
  if (collection.status === 'deployed') return ALREADY_DEPLOYED
  // A slug with no reservation time, or one older than 3 days, is lapsed; a deployed one never gets here.
  if (slugState(collection).kind !== 'reserved') return LINK_REQUIRED
  if (collection.max_supply === null) return SUPPLY_REQUIRED
  if (!rows.start) return NOT_RENDERED
  return null
}

/**
 * Builds the constructor from the collection row, the finished render and the creator's form, encodes
 * it, picks the salt and predicts the address (section 6.1). Nothing is written. The refusals are
 * the ones `collection_record_deploy` would raise, decided here first so the creator reads them
 * before the wallet opens.
 */
export async function prepareCore(ctx: DeployContext, form: DeployForm): Promise<DeployOutcome<Prepared>> {
  const { collection } = ctx
  const rows = await doneRows(ctx)
  const refusal = refusalBefore(ctx, rows)
  if (refusal) return { refused: refusal }
  // refusalBefore made these non-null; TypeScript cannot see it. A done job always has its base URI.
  if (!rows.start || rows.start.base_uri === null || collection.max_supply === null)
    return { refused: NOT_RENDERED }
  const valid = validateDeployForm(form, collection.max_supply)
  if (!valid.ok) return { refused: valid.error }
  const chainId = resolveDeployChain(rows.real?.quote_chain_id ?? null, valid.data.chainId)
  if (chainId === null) return { refused: WRONG_CHAIN }
  if (!chainById(chainId)) return { refused: CHAIN_NOT_READY }
  const factory = factoryOn(chainId)
  if (!factory) return { refused: CHAIN_NOT_READY }
  const deployer = getAddress(ctx.wallet)
  const args = deployArgs(
    { name: collection.name, symbol: collection.symbol, max_supply: collection.max_supply },
    { base_uri: rows.start.base_uri },
    valid.data,
    deployer,
  )
  const encodedArgs = encodeCollectionArgs(collectionArgsOf(args))
  const salt = collectionSalt(collection.id)
  const predicted = predictCollectionAddress({
    factory,
    deployer,
    salt,
    creationCode: COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE,
    args: encodedArgs,
  })
  const fees = await readCollectionFees(chainId)
  if (!fees) return { refused: FACTORY_UNREADABLE }
  return {
    data: {
      chainId,
      factory,
      deployer,
      salt,
      codeHash: COLLECTION_CODE_HASH,
      args,
      groups: valid.data.groups,
      encodedArgs,
      predicted,
      baseUri: rows.start.base_uri,
      revealsTo: revealsToOf(rows.start, rows.real),
      fees,
    },
  }
}

/** The numeric columns as text, so no wei is lost (PostgREST answers numeric as a JSON number). */
const DEPLOYMENT_COLUMNS =
  'collection_id, chain_id, contract_address, tx_hash, deployer, deployed_at, base_uri, payout_address, royalty_bps, royalty_receiver, public_max_per_wallet, mint_fee_flat_wei::text, mint_fee_bps, airdrop_fee_per_token_wei::text'

type DeploymentRow = {
  collection_id: string
  chain_id: number
  contract_address: string
  tx_hash: string
  deployer: string
  deployed_at: string
  base_uri: string
  payout_address: string
  royalty_bps: number
  royalty_receiver: string
  public_max_per_wallet: number
  mint_fee_flat_wei: string
  mint_fee_bps: number
  airdrop_fee_per_token_wei: string
}

type GroupRow = { onchain_id: number; name: string; price_wei: string; max_supply: number }

/** The collection's deployment and its launch phases under the creator's client, or null before deploy (section 6.3). */
export async function readDeployment(supabase: LockClient, collectionId: string): Promise<DeployRecord | null> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('deployments')
    .select(DEPLOYMENT_COLUMNS)
    .eq('collection_id', collectionId)
    .maybeSingle()
  if (error) throw new Error(`The deployment could not be read: ${error.message}`)
  if (!data) return null
  const row = data as unknown as DeploymentRow
  const groups = await supabase
    .from('mint_groups')
    .select('onchain_id, name, price_wei::text, max_supply')
    .eq('collection_id', collectionId)
    .order('onchain_id')
  if (groups.error) throw new Error(`The launch phases could not be read: ${groups.error.message}`)
  return {
    chainId: row.chain_id,
    contractAddress: getAddress(row.contract_address),
    txHash: row.tx_hash as Hex,
    deployer: getAddress(row.deployer),
    deployedAt: Math.floor(Date.parse(row.deployed_at) / 1000),
    baseUri: row.base_uri,
    payout: getAddress(row.payout_address),
    royaltyBps: row.royalty_bps,
    royaltyReceiver: getAddress(row.royalty_receiver),
    publicMaxPerWallet: row.public_max_per_wallet,
    fees: {
      mintFeeFlatWei: row.mint_fee_flat_wei,
      mintFeeBps: row.mint_fee_bps,
      airdropFeePerTokenWei: row.airdrop_fee_per_token_wei,
    },
    groups: ((groups.data ?? []) as unknown as GroupRow[]).map((group) => ({
      onchainId: group.onchain_id,
      name: group.name,
      priceWei: group.price_wei,
      maxSupply: group.max_supply,
    })),
  }
}

/**
 * Verifies a deployment from its receipt and records it (section 6.2). Mirrors `confirmCore`: the
 * address comes from the factory's `CollectionDeployed` log and is recorded only when it equals the
 * prediction rebuilt from the same inputs. A second confirm with the recorded hash answers the record unchanged.
 */
export async function confirmDeployCore(
  ctx: DeployContext,
  txHash: Hex,
  form: DeployForm,
): Promise<DeployOutcome<DeployRecord>> {
  const { supabase, collection } = ctx
  const hash = txHash.toLowerCase() as Hex
  const existing = await readDeployment(supabase, collection.id)
  if (existing) return existing.txHash === hash ? { data: existing } : { refused: ALREADY_DEPLOYED }
  const prepared = await prepareCore(ctx, form)
  if (!('data' in prepared)) return prepared
  const used = await supabase.from('deployments').select('collection_id').eq('tx_hash', hash).limit(1)
  if (used.error) return { error: used.error }
  if (used.data.length > 0) return { refused: DEPLOY_USED }

  const { chainId, factory, deployer, salt, codeHash, predicted, args, groups, fees, baseUri } = prepared.data
  let client: PublicClient
  try {
    client = publicClientFor(chainId)
  } catch {
    return { refused: CHAIN_NOT_READY }
  }
  let receipt: Awaited<ReturnType<PublicClient['getTransactionReceipt']>>
  try {
    receipt = await client.getTransactionReceipt({ hash })
  } catch (error) {
    // Not mined yet, or not visible to this node yet: the screen asks again in 3 seconds.
    if (!(error instanceof TransactionReceiptNotFoundError))
      console.error(`The receipt could not be read: ${errorSummary(error)}`)
    return { refused: DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED }
  }
  // sic: viem types the receipt as always present; a node can still answer with nothing.
  if (!receipt) return { refused: DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED }
  if (receipt.status === 'reverted') return { refused: DEPLOY_REVERTED }
  // A receipt too shallow to survive a reorg is not recorded yet; the screen keeps asking. cacheTime 0:
  // viem reuses a block number for the poll interval, which can read older than the receipt just fetched.
  try {
    const latest = await client.getBlockNumber({ cacheTime: 0 })
    if (!hasConfirmations(receipt.blockNumber, latest, confirmationsFor(chainId)))
      return { refused: DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`The latest block could not be read: ${errorSummary(error)}`)
    return { refused: DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED }
  }
  const match = matchCollectionDeployed(receipt, { factory, deployer, salt, codeHash, predicted })
  if (!match.ok) return { refused: match.error }

  const recorded = await ctx.service().rpc('collection_record_deploy', {
    p_collection_id: collection.id,
    p_owner: ctx.owner,
    p_chain_id: chainId,
    p_factory: factory.toLowerCase(),
    p_deployer: deployer.toLowerCase(),
    p_salt: salt,
    p_code_hash: codeHash,
    p_args: args as unknown as Json,
    p_contract_address: match.collection.toLowerCase(),
    p_tx_hash: hash,
    p_base_uri: baseUri,
    p_payout_address: args.payout.toLowerCase(),
    p_royalty_bps: args.royaltyBps,
    p_royalty_receiver: args.royaltyReceiver.toLowerCase(),
    p_public_max_per_wallet: Number(args.publicMaxPerWallet),
    // numeric: decimal strings, so no wei is lost; the generated types call numeric `number`.
    p_mint_fee_flat_wei: fees.mintFeeFlatWei as unknown as number,
    p_mint_fee_bps: fees.mintFeeBps,
    p_airdrop_fee_per_token_wei: fees.airdropFeePerTokenWei as unknown as number,
    p_groups: groups.map((group) => ({
      name: group.name,
      price_wei: group.priceWei,
      max_supply: group.maxSupply,
    })) as unknown as Json,
  })
  if (recorded.error) return { error: recorded.error }
  const record = await readDeployment(supabase, collection.id)
  if (!record) throw new Error('The deployment was recorded and cannot be read back.')
  return { data: record }
}

/** What the deploy page shows (section 7.2): the finished jobs, the paid chain, its fee snapshot and the record. */
export async function deployViewCore(ctx: DeployContext): Promise<DeployOutcome<DeployView>> {
  const rows = await doneRows(ctx)
  const [start, real, record] = await Promise.all([
    asDoneJob(ctx.supabase, rows.start),
    asDoneJob(ctx.supabase, rows.real),
    readDeployment(ctx.supabase, ctx.collection.id),
  ])
  const chainId = record?.chainId ?? rows.real?.quote_chain_id ?? null
  const fees = record ? record.fees : chainId !== null ? await readCollectionFees(chainId) : null
  return { data: { jobs: { start, real }, chainId, fees, record } }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/lib/deploy/deploy-write.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import { deployWriteError } from '@/lib/art/db-error'
import { errorSummary } from '@/lib/art/lock-core'
import { ownedCollection } from '@/lib/art/owned'
import { fail, THROWN, type ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { createServiceClient } from '@/lib/supabase/service'
import type { DeployContext, DeployOutcome } from './deploy-core'

/**
 * Runs a deploy step for the signed-in owner of a collection (lockWrite's counterpart for Deploy). Two
 * clients: the creator's RLS client for the session check, the owner read and every read, and the
 * service client for `collection_record_deploy`, whose arguments come from a verified receipt, called
 * with the verified user's id as `p_owner`. The service client is built on first use, after the
 * ownership check, so a step that never needs it works without the key. A refusal is returned as its
 * sentence; a database error becomes the creator's sentence (`deployWriteError`); anything thrown is
 * logged and reads "Try again".
 */
export async function deployWrite<T>(
  collectionId: string,
  run: (ctx: DeployContext) => Promise<DeployOutcome<T>>,
): Promise<ActionResult<T>> {
  try {
    const owned = await ownedCollection(collectionId)
    if (owned.error !== undefined) return fail(owned.error)
    const outcome = await run({
      supabase: owned.supabase,
      service: createServiceClient,
      owner: owned.user.id,
      wallet: owned.user.walletAddress,
      collection: owned.collection,
    })
    if ('refused' in outcome) return fail(outcome.refused)
    if ('error' in outcome) return fail(deployWriteError(outcome.error))
    return { ok: true, data: outcome.data }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`A deploy step threw. ${errorSummary(error)}`)
    return fail(THROWN)
  }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/app/actions/deploy.ts`:

```ts
'use server'

import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'
import { txHash as txHashSchema } from '@/lib/art/lock-schemas'
import type { ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { parse } from '@/lib/art/schemas'
import { deployFormShape } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-args'
import { confirmDeployCore, deployViewCore, prepareCore, readDeployment } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-core'
import type { DeployForm, DeployRecord, DeployView, Prepared } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'
import { deployWrite } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-write'

/**
 * Builds and encodes the constructor from the collection, the finished render and the form, and
 * predicts the address (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 6.3). Nothing is written. The core
 * validates the form again against the supply; the shape is checked here so a malformed payload is
 * refused before the owner read.
 */
export async function prepareDeploy(collectionId: string, form: DeployForm): Promise<ActionResult<Prepared>> {
  const input = parse(deployFormShape, form)
  if (!input.ok) return input
  return deployWrite(collectionId, (ctx) => prepareCore(ctx, form))
}

/** Checks a deployment against its receipt's CollectionDeployed log and the prediction, then records the contract. */
export async function confirmDeploy(
  collectionId: string,
  txHash: string,
  form: DeployForm,
): Promise<ActionResult<DeployRecord>> {
  const hash = parse(txHashSchema, txHash)
  if (!hash.ok) return hash
  const input = parse(deployFormShape, form)
  if (!input.ok) return input
  const result = await deployWrite(collectionId, (ctx) => confirmDeployCore(ctx, hash.data, form))
  // The collection is deployed: the stepper, Manage drop and every page under the collection change.
  if (result.ok) revalidatePath('/collections/[id]', 'layout')
  return result
}

/** The deployment record, or null before deploy. */
export async function getDeployment(collectionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<DeployRecord | null>> {
  return deployWrite(collectionId, async (ctx) => ({ data: await readDeployment(ctx.supabase, ctx.collection.id) }))
}

/** What the deploy page shows: the finished jobs, the paid chain, its fee snapshot and the record. */
export async function getDeployView(collectionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<DeployView>> {
  return deployWrite(collectionId, (ctx) => deployViewCore(ctx))
}
```
