### Task 5: verify:deploy against local Supabase and Anvil

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/web/scripts/verify-deploy.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/package.json` (one script line)
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md` (one command line)
- Test: `pnpm --filter @cobweb/web verify:deploy`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `localConfig`, `adminClient`, `clientOptions`, `LocalClient` from `./render-deps.ts`; `lockCore`, `confirmCore`, `LockContext` from `../src/lib/art/lock-core.ts`; `readLockCollection`; `prepareCore`, `confirmDeployCore`, `readDeployment`, `DeployContext` from `../src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts` (Task 4); `collectionArgsOf` (Task 4); `collectionSalt`, `encodeCollectionArgs`, `predictCollectionAddress`, `factoryAbi` from `@cobweb/shared` (Tasks 1 and 2); `COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE`, `COLLECTION_CODE_HASH`, `collectionAbi` from `@cobweb/shared/collection` (Task 1); the sentences from `../src/lib/art/result.ts` (Task 4).
- Produces: the script `verify:deploy` (15 PASS lines) and its CLAUDE.md line.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the script**

Create `apps/web/scripts/verify-deploy.ts`:

```ts
// Checks Deploy against a LOCAL Anvil chain and LOCAL Supabase (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md,
// section 10): a signed-in creator's generative collection is locked, paid and rendered to done through
// the same cores and functions the app uses; prepareCore answers the paid chain, the prediction (which
// the factory's own view agrees with) and the fees; the script wallet sends deployCollection with the
// prepared bytes; confirmDeployCore records the contract, the launch phases and the permanent link from
// the factory's event; the contract's views match the arguments; then the refusals: another chain, a
// draft with nothing rendered, an unrelated transaction, a contract deployed with other arguments, a
// second confirm (idempotent), a deployed collection, a hash recorded for another collection; and a
// deferred collection deploying on its placeholder base URI. Fifteen PASS lines with the cleanup.
//
//   supabase start
//   pnpm --filter @cobweb/web chain:local    (then copy its two lines into apps/web/.env.local)
//   pnpm --filter @cobweb/web verify:deploy
//
// Settings come from scripts/render-deps.ts (the environment, `supabase status -o env` and
// apps/web/.env.local); it refuses non-local endpoints. Never prints a key. Deletes every row it
// creates. Runs under `node --conditions=react-server --import ./scripts/resolve-ts.mjs`.

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import {
  createTestClient,
  createWalletClient,
  http,
  parseEther,
  publicActions,
  type Hex,
} from 'viem'
import { generatePrivateKey, privateKeyToAccount, type PrivateKeyAccount } from 'viem/accounts'
import { anvil } from 'viem/chains'
import { createSiweMessage, generateSiweNonce } from 'viem/siwe'
import type { RenderQueue } from '@cobweb/shared/render-queue'
import type { Database } from '../src/lib/database.types.ts'
import { adminClient, clientOptions, localConfig, type LocalClient } from './render-deps.ts'

// Every local setting, with non-local endpoints refused (scripts/render-deps.ts). It also fills the
// environment the shared chain clients read (RPC_URL_31337, FACTORY_ADDRESS_31337).
const settings = localConfig()
const { url, publishableKey, rpc, factory } = settings

// Imported after the environment is set, so the shared clients read the local chain settings.
const { collectionSalt, encodeCollectionArgs, factoryAbi, predictCollectionAddress } =
  await import('@cobweb/shared')
const { COLLECTION_CODE_HASH, COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE, collectionAbi } =
  await import('@cobweb/shared/collection')
const { confirmCore, lockCore } = await import('../src/lib/art/lock-core.ts')
const { readLockCollection } = await import('../src/lib/art/lock-collection.ts')
const { confirmDeployCore, prepareCore, readDeployment } = await import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts')
const { collectionArgsOf } = await import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-args.ts')
const { ALREADY_DEPLOYED, DEPLOY_USED, NOT_RENDERED, NOT_THIS_DEPLOY, OTHER_CONTRACT, WRONG_CHAIN } =
  await import('../src/lib/art/result.ts')
type LockContext = import('../src/lib/art/lock-core.ts').LockContext
type DeployContext = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts').DeployContext
type DeployOutcome<T> = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-core.ts').DeployOutcome<T>
type DeployForm = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-types.ts').DeployForm
type Prepared = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-types.ts').Prepared
type DeployRecord = import('../src/lib/deploy/deploy-types.ts').DeployRecord

const admin: LocalClient = adminClient(settings)
const chain = createTestClient({ chain: anvil, mode: 'anvil', transport: http(rpc) }).extend(publicActions)
/** A queue that sends nothing: the render is driven by hand below, not by a worker. */
const silentQueue: RenderQueue = { send: async () => {} }
const CID = (letter: string) => `bafybei${letter.repeat(52)}`

let passed = 0
const failed: string[] = []
async function check(name: string, fn: () => Promise<string | null>): Promise<void> {
  let problem: string | null
  try {
    problem = await fn()
  } catch (e) {
    problem = `threw ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`
  }
  if (problem === null) {
    passed++
    console.log(`PASS ${name}`)
  } else {
    failed.push(name)
    console.log(`FAIL ${name}: ${problem}`)
  }
}

const userIds: string[] = []
const collectionIds: string[] = []

async function signIn(account: PrivateKeyAccount) {
  const client: LocalClient = createClient<Database>(url, publishableKey, clientOptions)
  const message = createSiweMessage({
    address: account.address,
    chainId: 1,
    domain: 'localhost:3000',
    nonce: generateSiweNonce(),
    uri: 'http://localhost:3000',
    version: '1',
    statement: 'Sign in to Cobweb.',
    issuedAt: new Date(),
  })
  const signature = await account.signMessage({ message })
  const { data, error } = await client.auth.signInWithWeb3({ chain: 'ethereum', message, signature })
  if (error || !data.user) throw new Error(`sign-in failed: ${error?.message ?? 'no user'}`)
  userIds.push(data.user.id)
  return { client, userId: data.user.id }
}

const HEX64 = (n: number) => n.toString(16).padStart(64, '0')
let fileCount = 0

/** A generative collection of three tokens over two layers of two traits, owned by the client's creator. */
async function seededCollection(client: LocalClient, name: string): Promise<string> {
  const { data: col, error } = await client
    .from('collections')
    .insert({ name: `Deploy check ${name} ${Date.now()} ${++fileCount}`, symbol: 'DPL', project_type: 'generative' })
    .select('id')
    .single()
  if (error || !col) throw new Error(`collection: ${error?.message ?? 'no row'}`)
  const cid = col.id
  collectionIds.push(cid)
  const art = await client
    .from('art_collections')
    .insert({ collection_id: cid, name: 'Owls', size: 3, position: 0 })
    .select('id')
    .single()
  if (art.error || !art.data) throw new Error(`art collection: ${art.error?.message ?? 'no row'}`)
  const artId = art.data.id
  const traits: string[][] = []
  for (const [position, layerName] of ['Background', 'Eyes'].entries()) {
    const layer = await client
      .from('layers')
      .insert({ collection_id: cid, art_collection_id: artId, name: layerName, position })
      .select('id')
      .single()
    if (layer.error || !layer.data) throw new Error(`layer: ${layer.error?.message ?? 'no row'}`)
    const ids: string[] = []
    for (const [index, traitName] of ['One', 'Two'].entries()) {
      const hash = HEX64(++fileCount)
      const upload = await client.rpc('record_upload', {
        p_collection_id: cid,
        p_kind: 'layer_image',
        p_storage_key: `${cid}/layers/${hash}.png`,
        p_content_hash: hash,
        p_mime: 'image/png',
        p_media_type: 'image',
        p_bytes: 1234,
        p_width: 1000,
        p_height: 1000,
        p_file_name: `${traitName}.png`,
      })
      if (upload.error) throw new Error(`upload: ${upload.error.message}`)
      const trait = await client
        .from('traits')
        .insert({
          collection_id: cid,
          layer_id: layer.data.id,
          name: traitName,
          weight: 50,
          position: index,
          upload_id: upload.data as string,
        })
        .select('id')
        .single()
      if (trait.error || !trait.data) throw new Error(`trait: ${trait.error?.message ?? 'no row'}`)
      ids.push(trait.data.id)
    }
    traits.push(ids)
  }
  const [background, eyes] = traits as [string[], string[]]
  const dna = [
    [background[0]!, eyes[0]!],
    [background[0]!, eyes[1]!],
    [background[1]!, eyes[0]!],
  ]
  const tokens = await client.from('tokens').insert(
    dna.map((traitIds, index) => ({
      collection_id: cid,
      token_id: index + 1,
      art_collection_id: artId,
      dna: traitIds,
    })),
  )
  if (tokens.error) throw new Error(`tokens: ${tokens.error.message}`)
  return cid
}

/** The context `lockWrite` builds, re-read from the row each time so the status is current. */
async function lockContextFor(client: LocalClient, owner: string, wallet: string, cid: string): Promise<LockContext> {
  const collection = await readLockCollection(client, cid)
  if (!collection || collection.owner_id !== owner) throw new Error('the creator cannot read the collection')
  return { supabase: client, service: () => admin, owner, wallet: wallet.toLowerCase(), collection, queue: silentQueue }
}

/** The context `deployWrite` builds: the same read, without the queue. */
async function deployContextFor(client: LocalClient, owner: string, wallet: string, cid: string): Promise<DeployContext> {
  const { queue: _queue, ...context } = await lockContextFor(client, owner, wallet, cid)
  void _queue
  return context
}

const refusal = <T>(outcome: DeployOutcome<T>) =>
  'refused' in outcome ? outcome.refused : 'error' in outcome ? `db ${outcome.error?.code}` : 'accepted'

async function pay(account: PrivateKeyAccount, jobId: Hex, value: bigint): Promise<Hex> {
  const wallet = createWalletClient({ account, chain: anvil, transport: http(rpc) })
  const hash = await wallet.writeContract({ address: factory, abi: factoryAbi, functionName: 'payForArt', args: [jobId], value })
  const receipt = await chain.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash })
  if (receipt.status !== 'success') throw new Error(`payForArt reverted: ${hash}`)
  return hash
}

/** The render functions with the service role, from a paid (or placeholder) job to done, as the worker would. */
async function finishRender(
  jobId: string,
  imagesCid: string,
  metadataCid: string,
  baseUri: string,
  placeholderImageCid: string | null = null,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const steps: [string, Record<string, unknown>][] = [
    ['art_claim_render', { p_job_id: jobId }],
    ['art_batch_start', { p_job_id: jobId, p_index: 0 }],
    ['art_batch_done', { p_job_id: jobId, p_index: 0, p_images_cid: imagesCid, p_images_size: 1000 }],
    [
      'art_render_finish',
      { p_job_id: jobId, p_metadata_cid: metadataCid, p_base_uri: baseUri, p_placeholder_image_cid: placeholderImageCid },
    ],
  ]
  for (const [fn, args] of steps) {
    const { error } = await admin.rpc(fn as 'art_claim_render', args as never)
    if (error) return `${fn}: ${error.message}`
  }
  return null
}

/** Sends deployCollection with the shipped creation code, the given arguments and salt, and waits for the receipt. */
async function deploy(account: PrivateKeyAccount, encodedArgs: Hex, salt: Hex): Promise<Hex> {
  const wallet = createWalletClient({ account, chain: anvil, transport: http(rpc) })
  const hash = await wallet.writeContract({
    address: factory,
    abi: factoryAbi,
    functionName: 'deployCollection',
    args: [COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE, encodedArgs, salt],
  })
  const receipt = await chain.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash })
  if (receipt.status !== 'success') throw new Error(`deployCollection reverted: ${hash}`)
  return hash
}

type CollectionState = { status: string; chain_id: number | null; contract_address: string | null; slug: string | null; slug_reserved_at: string | null }

async function collectionState(cid: string): Promise<CollectionState> {
  const { data, error } = await admin
    .from('collections')
    .select('status, chain_id, contract_address, slug, slug_reserved_at')
    .eq('id', cid)
    .single()
  if (error) throw new Error(`collection state: ${error.message}`)
  return data
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  console.log(`Verifying deploy on ${rpc} against ${url}`)
  await check('1. chain: Anvil answers as 31337 and the factory approves the shipped creation code hash', async () => {
    if ((await chain.getChainId()) !== 31337) return 'not chain 31337'
    const code = await chain.getCode({ address: factory })
    if (!code || code === '0x') return `no contract at ${factory}; run chain:local again`
    const approved = await chain.readContract({
      address: factory,
      abi: factoryAbi,
      functionName: 'isCreationCodeApproved',
      args: [COLLECTION_CODE_HASH],
    })
    return approved
      ? null
      : `the factory does not approve ${COLLECTION_CODE_HASH}; run forge build, the copy script and chain:local again`
  })

  const creator = privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey())
  const stranger = privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey())
  await chain.setBalance({ address: creator.address, value: parseEther('10') })
  await chain.setBalance({ address: stranger.address, value: parseEther('10') })
  const { client, userId } = await signIn(creator)
  const slugFor = (cid: string) => `deploy-${cid.slice(0, 8)}`

  // The rendered collection.
  const rendered = await seededCollection(client, 'rendered')
  await check('2. seed: a generative collection is locked, paid, rendered to done and linked', async () => {
    const lockCtx = await lockContextFor(client, userId, creator.address, rendered)
    const locked = await lockCore(lockCtx, { allowDuplicates: false, chainId: 31337 })
    if (!('data' in locked)) return `lock: ${refusal(locked)}`
    const job = locked.data
    const paidHash = await pay(creator, job.jobId, BigInt(job.quote.totalWei))
    const confirmed = await confirmCore(lockCtx, job.id, paidHash)
    if (!('data' in confirmed)) return `pay: ${refusal(confirmed)}`
    if (confirmed.data.state !== 'paid') return `job ${confirmed.data.state}`
    const rendering = await finishRender(job.id, CID('d'), CID('e'), `ipfs://${CID('e')}/`)
    if (rendering) return rendering
    const link = await client.rpc('reserve_slug', { p_collection_id: rendered, p_slug: slugFor(rendered) })
    if (link.error) return `link: ${link.error.message}`
    const state = await collectionState(rendered)
    return state.status === 'rendered' && state.slug === slugFor(rendered) ? null : JSON.stringify(state)
  })

  const form: DeployForm = {
    chainId: 31337,
    payout: creator.address,
    royaltyBps: 500,
    royaltyReceiver: stranger.address,
    publicMaxPerWallet: 2,
    groups: [
      { name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 2 },
      { name: 'Holders', priceWei: '1000000000000000', maxSupply: 1 },
    ],
  }
  let prepared: Prepared | null = null
  let ctx = await deployContextFor(client, userId, creator.address, rendered)

  await check(
    '3. prepareCore: answers the paid chain, the prediction the factory agrees with, the base URI and the fees, and refuses another chain',
    async () => {
      const outcome = await prepareCore(ctx, form)
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return refusal(outcome)
      prepared = outcome.data
      const fromFactory = await chain.readContract({
        address: factory,
        abi: factoryAbi,
        functionName: 'predictCollectionAddress',
        args: [creator.address, COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE, prepared.encodedArgs, prepared.salt],
      })
      const [flat, bps, airdrop] = await chain.readContract({ address: factory, abi: factoryAbi, functionName: 'collectionFees' })
      const problems = [
        prepared.chainId === 31337 ? null : `chain ${prepared.chainId}`,
        prepared.factory === factory ? null : `factory ${prepared.factory}`,
        prepared.deployer === creator.address ? null : `deployer ${prepared.deployer}`,
        prepared.salt === collectionSalt(rendered) ? null : `salt ${prepared.salt}`,
        prepared.codeHash === COLLECTION_CODE_HASH ? null : `code hash ${prepared.codeHash}`,
        prepared.predicted === fromFactory ? null : `predicted ${prepared.predicted}, the factory says ${fromFactory}`,
        prepared.baseUri === `ipfs://${CID('e')}/` ? null : `base URI ${prepared.baseUri}`,
        prepared.revealsTo === null ? null : `reveals to ${prepared.revealsTo}`,
        prepared.fees.mintFeeFlatWei === flat.toString() &&
        prepared.fees.mintFeeBps === Number(bps) &&
        prepared.fees.airdropFeePerTokenWei === airdrop.toString()
          ? null
          : `fees ${JSON.stringify(prepared.fees)}`,
        prepared.args.name !== '' && prepared.args.symbol === 'DPL' && prepared.args.maxSupply === '3'
          ? null
          : `args ${JSON.stringify(prepared.args)}`,
      ].filter(Boolean)
      if (problems.length) return problems.join('; ')
      const elsewhere = await prepareCore(ctx, { ...form, chainId: 1 })
      return refusal(elsewhere) === WRONG_CHAIN ? null : `another chain: ${refusal(elsewhere)}`
    },
  )

  await check('4. prepareCore: a draft collection with nothing rendered is refused', async () => {
    const draft = await seededCollection(client, 'draft')
    const supply = await client.from('collections').update({ max_supply: 3 }).eq('id', draft)
    if (supply.error) return `supply: ${supply.error.message}`
    const link = await client.rpc('reserve_slug', { p_collection_id: draft, p_slug: slugFor(draft) })
    if (link.error) return `link: ${link.error.message}`
    const outcome = await prepareCore(await deployContextFor(client, userId, creator.address, draft), form)
    return refusal(outcome) === NOT_RENDERED ? null : refusal(outcome)
  })

  await check('5. confirmDeployCore: a transaction that deployed nothing is refused', async () => {
    const wallet = createWalletClient({ account: creator, chain: anvil, transport: http(rpc) })
    const hash = await wallet.sendTransaction({ to: stranger.address, value: 1n })
    await chain.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash })
    const outcome = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, hash, form)
    return refusal(outcome) === NOT_THIS_DEPLOY ? null : refusal(outcome)
  })

  let otherHash: Hex | null = null
  await check(
    '6. confirmDeployCore: a contract deployed from the prepared bytes with other arguments is refused and never recorded',
    async () => {
      if (!prepared) return 'not prepared'
      const otherArgs = encodeCollectionArgs(collectionArgsOf({ ...prepared.args, royaltyBps: 600 }))
      otherHash = await deploy(creator, otherArgs, prepared.salt)
      const outcome = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, otherHash, form)
      if (refusal(outcome) !== OTHER_CONTRACT) return refusal(outcome)
      const record = await readDeployment(client, rendered)
      if (record) return `recorded ${record.contractAddress}`
      return (await collectionState(rendered)).status === 'rendered' ? null : 'the collection changed'
    },
  )

  let deployHash: Hex | null = null
  await check(
    '7. deploy: the script wallet sends deployCollection with the prepared bytes and the factory records the predicted address',
    async () => {
      if (!prepared) return 'not prepared'
      deployHash = await deploy(creator, prepared.encodedArgs, prepared.salt)
      const isCollection = await chain.readContract({
        address: factory,
        abi: factoryAbi,
        functionName: 'isCollection',
        args: [prepared.predicted],
      })
      return isCollection ? null : `the factory does not know ${prepared.predicted}`
    },
  )

  let record: DeployRecord | null = null
  await check(
    '8. confirmDeployCore: records the contract, the launch phases and the permanent link; the collection reads deployed on its chain',
    async () => {
      if (!prepared || !deployHash) return 'not deployed'
      const outcome = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, deployHash, form)
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return refusal(outcome)
      record = outcome.data
      const state = await collectionState(rendered)
      const problems = [
        record.chainId === 31337 ? null : `chain ${record.chainId}`,
        record.contractAddress === prepared.predicted ? null : `address ${record.contractAddress}`,
        record.txHash === deployHash.toLowerCase() ? null : `hash ${record.txHash}`,
        record.deployer === creator.address ? null : `deployer ${record.deployer}`,
        record.baseUri === prepared.baseUri ? null : `base URI ${record.baseUri}`,
        record.payout === creator.address && record.royaltyReceiver === stranger.address && record.royaltyBps === 500
          ? null
          : 'payout or royalty',
        record.publicMaxPerWallet === 2 ? null : `cap ${record.publicMaxPerWallet}`,
        JSON.stringify(record.fees) === JSON.stringify(prepared.fees) ? null : `fees ${JSON.stringify(record.fees)}`,
        JSON.stringify(record.groups) ===
        JSON.stringify([
          { onchainId: 0, name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 2 },
          { onchainId: 1, name: 'Holders', priceWei: '1000000000000000', maxSupply: 1 },
        ])
          ? null
          : `groups ${JSON.stringify(record.groups)}`,
        state.status === 'deployed' && state.chain_id === 31337 ? null : `collection ${JSON.stringify(state)}`,
        state.contract_address === prepared.predicted.toLowerCase() ? null : `contract ${state.contract_address}`,
        state.slug === slugFor(rendered) && state.slug_reserved_at === null ? null : `link ${state.slug} ${state.slug_reserved_at}`,
      ].filter(Boolean)
      return problems.length ? problems.join('; ') : null
    },
  )

  await check(
    '9. contract: name, symbol, maxSupply, baseURI, owner, payout, publicMaxMintPerWallet, royaltyInfo and collectionState match the arguments',
    async () => {
      if (!prepared) return 'not prepared'
      const address = prepared.predicted
      const name = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'name' })
      const symbol = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'symbol' })
      const maxSupply = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'maxSupply' })
      const baseURI = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'baseURI' })
      const owner = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'owner' })
      const payout = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'ownerPayoutAddress' })
      const cap = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'publicMaxMintPerWallet' })
      const [receiver, royalty] = await chain.readContract({
        address,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'royaltyInfo',
        args: [1n, 10_000n],
      })
      const state = await chain.readContract({ address, abi: collectionAbi, functionName: 'collectionState' })
      const problems = [
        name === prepared.args.name ? null : `name ${name}`,
        symbol === 'DPL' ? null : `symbol ${symbol}`,
        maxSupply === 3n ? null : `maxSupply ${maxSupply}`,
        baseURI === prepared.baseUri ? null : `baseURI ${baseURI}`,
        owner === creator.address ? null : `owner ${owner}`,
        payout === creator.address ? null : `payout ${payout}`,
        cap === 2n ? null : `cap ${cap}`,
        receiver === stranger.address && royalty === 500n ? null : `royalty ${receiver} ${royalty}`,
        state.groups.length === 2 &&
        state.groups[0]?.price === 0n &&
        state.groups[0]?.maxSupply === 2n &&
        state.groups[1]?.price === 1_000_000_000_000_000n &&
        state.groups[1]?.maxSupply === 1n &&
        state.groups.every((group) => !group.active && !group.activeFlag)
          ? null
          : `groups ${JSON.stringify(state.groups, (_k, v) => (typeof v === 'bigint' ? v.toString() : v))}`,
        state.maxSupply === 3n && state.totalMinted === 0n ? null : 'state supply',
      ].filter(Boolean)
      return problems.length ? problems.join('; ') : null
    },
  )

  await check('10. confirmDeployCore: a second confirm with the same hash answers the record unchanged', async () => {
    if (!deployHash || !record) return 'not recorded'
    ctx = await deployContextFor(client, userId, creator.address, rendered)
    const again = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, deployHash, form)
    if (!('data' in again)) return refusal(again)
    return JSON.stringify(again.data) === JSON.stringify(record) ? null : `changed: ${JSON.stringify(again.data)}`
  })

  await check('11. prepareCore and confirmDeployCore: a deployed collection is refused as already deployed', async () => {
    if (!otherHash) return 'no other hash'
    const prepare = await prepareCore(ctx, form)
    if (refusal(prepare) !== ALREADY_DEPLOYED) return `prepare: ${refusal(prepare)}`
    const confirm = await confirmDeployCore(ctx, otherHash, form)
    return refusal(confirm) === ALREADY_DEPLOYED ? null : `confirm: ${refusal(confirm)}`
  })

  // The deferred collection.
  let deferred = ''
  await check('12. seed: a deferred collection with its placeholder rendered, a supply and a live link', async () => {
    const { data: col, error } = await client
      .from('collections')
      .insert({ name: `Deploy check deferred ${Date.now()}`, symbol: 'DPL', project_type: 'generative' })
      .select('id')
      .single()
    if (error || !col) return `collection: ${error?.message ?? 'no row'}`
    deferred = col.id
    collectionIds.push(deferred)
    const set = await client.from('collections').update({ art_deferred: true, max_supply: 5 }).eq('id', deferred)
    if (set.error) return `deferred: ${set.error.message}`
    const hash = HEX64(++fileCount)
    const upload = await client.rpc('record_upload', {
      p_collection_id: deferred,
      p_kind: 'placeholder',
      p_storage_key: `${deferred}/placeholder/${hash}.png`,
      p_content_hash: hash,
      p_mime: 'image/png',
      p_media_type: 'image',
      p_bytes: 1234,
      p_width: 1000,
      p_height: 1000,
      p_file_name: 'placeholder.png',
    })
    if (upload.error) return `upload: ${upload.error.message}`
    const lock = await client.rpc('art_lock_placeholder', {
      p_collection_id: deferred,
      p_placeholder_upload_id: upload.data as string,
    })
    if (lock.error) return `placeholder lock: ${lock.error.message}`
    const jobId = (lock.data as { id: string }).id
    const rendering = await finishRender(jobId, CID('p'), CID('q'), `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json`, CID('p'))
    if (rendering) return rendering
    const link = await client.rpc('reserve_slug', { p_collection_id: deferred, p_slug: slugFor(deferred) })
    if (link.error) return `link: ${link.error.message}`
    const ready = await client.rpc('collection_placeholder_ready', { p_collection_id: deferred })
    if (ready.error) return `ready: ${ready.error.message}`
    return ready.data === true ? null : 'the placeholder is not ready'
  })

  const deferredForm: DeployForm = { ...form, groups: [{ name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 5 }] }
  await check('13. confirmDeployCore: a transaction recorded for another collection is refused', async () => {
    if (!deployHash) return 'not deployed'
    const dctx = await deployContextFor(client, userId, creator.address, deferred)
    const outcome = await confirmDeployCore(dctx, deployHash, deferredForm)
    if (refusal(outcome) !== DEPLOY_USED) return refusal(outcome)
    return (await collectionState(deferred)).status === 'draft' ? null : 'the collection changed'
  })

  await check(
    '14. deploy: the deferred collection deploys on the chain the creator picked, on its placeholder base URI, with nothing to reveal to',
    async () => {
      const dctx = await deployContextFor(client, userId, creator.address, deferred)
      const outcome = await prepareCore(dctx, deferredForm)
      if (!('data' in outcome)) return `prepare: ${refusal(outcome)}`
      const dprepared = outcome.data
      if (dprepared.chainId !== 31337) return `chain ${dprepared.chainId}`
      if (dprepared.baseUri !== `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json`) return `base URI ${dprepared.baseUri}`
      if (dprepared.revealsTo !== null) return `reveals to ${dprepared.revealsTo}`
      const expected = predictCollectionAddress({
        factory,
        deployer: creator.address,
        salt: collectionSalt(deferred),
        creationCode: COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE,
        args: dprepared.encodedArgs,
      })
      if (dprepared.predicted !== expected) return `predicted ${dprepared.predicted}, expected ${expected}`
      const hash = await deploy(creator, dprepared.encodedArgs, dprepared.salt)
      const confirmed = await confirmDeployCore(dctx, hash, deferredForm)
      if (!('data' in confirmed)) return `confirm: ${refusal(confirmed)}`
      const onChain = await chain.readContract({
        address: confirmed.data.contractAddress,
        abi: collectionAbi,
        functionName: 'baseURI',
      })
      const state = await collectionState(deferred)
      return confirmed.data.contractAddress === dprepared.predicted &&
        onChain === `ipfs://${CID('q')}/metadata.json` &&
        state.status === 'deployed' &&
        state.chain_id === 31337 &&
        confirmed.data.groups.length === 1
        ? null
        : `${JSON.stringify(confirmed.data)} ${onChain} ${JSON.stringify(state)}`
    },
  )
}

async function cleanup(): Promise<void> {
  await check('15. cleanup: the run leaves no job, deployment, collection or user behind', async () => {
    if (collectionIds.length) {
      const jobs = await admin.from('render_jobs').delete().in('collection_id', collectionIds)
      if (jobs.error) return `jobs: ${jobs.error.message}`
      const cols = await admin.from('collections').delete().in('id', collectionIds)
      if (cols.error) return `collections: ${cols.error.message}`
      const left = await admin.from('deployments').select('collection_id').in('collection_id', collectionIds)
      if (left.error) return `deployments: ${left.error.message}`
      if (left.data.length) return `${left.data.length} deployment(s) left`
    }
    for (const id of userIds) {
      const { error } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(id)
      if (error) return error.message
    }
    return null
  })
}

try {
  await main()
} catch (e) {
  failed.push('run')
  console.log(`FAIL run: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`)
} finally {
  await cleanup()
}

console.log(`\n${passed} passed, ${failed.length} failed`)
process.exit(failed.length ? 1 : 0)
```

One note on the code above: `finishRender` calls `admin.rpc(fn as 'art_claim_render', args as never)`. The cast lets one loop call four functions with different argument shapes through the typed client; the functions themselves check their arguments, and verify:local covers each of them on its own.

3. Replace the whole `frozenDetailFields` function (its doc comment included):

```ts
/**
 * Detail fields the database will refuse to change in this status
 * (the `collections_guard_frozen_fields` trigger). The project type never changes after creation.
 */
export function frozenDetailFields(
  status: CollectionStatus,
): ReadonlySet<'name' | 'symbol' | 'maxSupply' | 'projectType'> {
  const frozen = new Set<'name' | 'symbol' | 'maxSupply' | 'projectType'>(['projectType'])
  if (status !== 'draft') frozen.add('maxSupply')
  if (status === 'deployed') {
    frozen.add('name')
    frozen.add('symbol')
  }
  return frozen
}
```

with

```ts
export type DetailField = 'name' | 'symbol' | 'description' | 'maxSupply' | 'projectType'

/**
 * Detail fields the database will refuse to change for this row (the `collections_guard_frozen_fields`
 * trigger). The project type never changes after creation; the supply freezes after draft; the name,
 * symbol and description freeze while the art is locked (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section
 * 4.3: the metadata the worker writes and the contract's name must agree); the name and symbol stay
 * frozen once deployed, including a collection whose placeholder alone was locked (no art lock).
 */
export function frozenDetailFields(collection: {
  status: CollectionStatus
  art_locked_at: string | null
}): ReadonlySet<DetailField> {
  const frozen = new Set<DetailField>(['projectType'])
  if (collection.status !== 'draft') frozen.add('maxSupply')
  if (collection.art_locked_at !== null) {
    frozen.add('name')
    frozen.add('symbol')
    frozen.add('description')
  }
  if (collection.status === 'deployed') {
    frozen.add('name')
    frozen.add('symbol')
  }
  return frozen
}
```


3. Before `/** The page for a stage that cannot be opened yet. */` add:

```ts
/**
 * Whether a deferred collection's placeholder job is done, which opens Deploy while the collection is
 * still a draft (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 9): one `collection_placeholder_ready` call
 * under the creator's session. False for a collection that is not deferred, and in sample mode, where
 * every stage is open anyway. The deploy page and the collection layout pass the answer to
 * `stageContext` and the stepper.
 */
export async function placeholderReady(
  collection: Pick<Tables<'collections'>, 'id' | 'art_deferred'>,
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (isDemoMode() || !collection.art_deferred) return false
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('collection_placeholder_ready', { p_collection_id: collection.id })
  if (error) throw new Error(`The placeholder readiness could not be read: ${error.message}`)
  return data === true
}

```

Replace `apps/web/src/app/actions/collection-details.ts` with:

```ts
'use server'

import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'
import { getVerifiedUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import { updateDetailsSchema, type DetailsField } from '@/lib/collection-details'
import { frozenDetailFields } from '@/lib/steps'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'

export type UpdateDetailsState = {
  /** True after a successful save; cleared by the next submit. */
  saved?: boolean
  formError?: string
  fieldErrors?: Partial<Record<DetailsField, string>>
}

/** A form field as text. A missing field becomes '', so validation gives its own message. */
function text(formData: FormData, name: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(name)
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''
}

const DEPLOYED_MESSAGE: Record<'name' | 'symbol' | 'description', string> = {
  name: 'The name cannot change after deployment.',
  symbol: 'The symbol cannot change after deployment.',
  description: 'The description cannot change after deployment.',
}
/** The database's own sentence (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 4.3). */
const LOCKED_MESSAGE = 'The name, symbol and description cannot change while the art is locked.'

/**
 * Saves a collection's details (name, symbol, description). The supply is not set here: see
 * `setCollectionSupply`. Bind the collection id: `updateCollectionDetails.bind(null, id)`.
 * Checks the session with the auth server and that the signed-in creator owns the collection,
 * and only writes fields the row still allows (the database enforces the same rules in
 * `collections_guard_frozen_fields`: the three freeze while the art is locked, the name and symbol
 * once deployed).
 */
export async function updateCollectionDetails(
  collectionId: string,
  _previous: UpdateDetailsState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<UpdateDetailsState> {
  const user = await getVerifiedUser()
  if (!user) return { formError: 'Your session has ended. Sign in again to continue.' }
  if (!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(collectionId)) return { formError: 'This collection could not be found.' }

  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: current } = await supabase
    .from('collections')
    .select('id, owner_id, status, art_locked_at, name, symbol, description')
    .eq('id', collectionId)
    .maybeSingle()
  if (!current || current.owner_id !== user.id) return { formError: 'This collection could not be found.' }

  const parsed = updateDetailsSchema.safeParse({
    name: text(formData, 'name'),
    symbol: text(formData, 'symbol'),
    description: text(formData, 'description'),
  })
  if (!parsed.success) {
    const fieldErrors: NonNullable<UpdateDetailsState['fieldErrors']> = {}
    for (const issue of parsed.error.issues) {
      const field = issue.path[0] as DetailsField
      fieldErrors[field] ??= issue.message
    }
    return { fieldErrors }
  }
  const input = parsed.data

  // Frozen fields are not written. Asking to change one is an error, not a silent no-op.
  const frozen = frozenDetailFields(current)
  const stored = { name: current.name, symbol: current.symbol, description: current.description }
  const fieldErrors: NonNullable<UpdateDetailsState['fieldErrors']> = {}
  for (const field of ['name', 'symbol', 'description'] as const) {
    if (frozen.has(field) && input[field] !== stored[field])
      fieldErrors[field] = current.art_locked_at !== null ? LOCKED_MESSAGE : DEPLOYED_MESSAGE[field]
  }
  if (Object.keys(fieldErrors).length > 0) return { fieldErrors }

  const update: { name?: string; symbol?: string; description?: string } = {}
  if (!frozen.has('name')) update.name = input.name
  if (!frozen.has('symbol')) update.symbol = input.symbol
  if (!frozen.has('description')) update.description = input.description
  // Every field frozen and unchanged: nothing to write, and the trigger would refuse nothing either.
  if (Object.keys(update).length === 0) return { saved: true }

  const { error } = await supabase.from('collections').update(update).eq('id', collectionId)
  if (error) {
    // 23514 is a check violation, raised by the frozen-field trigger if the row changed meanwhile.
    if (error.code === '23514')
      return { formError: 'Some of these details can no longer change. Reload the page.' }
    return { formError: 'The details could not be saved. Try again.' }
  }

  // The workspace layout shows the name, so refresh it and every page under it.
  revalidatePath('/collections/[id]', 'layout')
  revalidatePath('/collections')
  return { saved: true }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/components/deploy/deploy-machine.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { Hex } from 'viem'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import {
  ALREADY_DEPLOYED,
  CHAIN_NOT_READY,
  DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED,
  DEPLOY_REVERTED,
  DEPLOY_TIMED_OUT,
  DEPLOY_USED,
  LINK_REQUIRED,
  NOT_FOUND,
  NOT_THIS_DEPLOY,
  OTHER_CODE,
  OTHER_CONTRACT,
  SIGNED_OUT,
  THROWN,
  UNSAVED,
  type ActionResult,
} from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { DeployForm, DeployRecord, Prepared } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'
import {
  deployReducer,
  forgetDeploy,
  pendingDeploy,
  rememberDeploy,
  retryHash,
  runDeploy,
  type DeployDeps,
  type DeployEvent,
  type DeployInput,
  type KeyStore,
  type PendingDeploy,
} from './deploy-machine'
import type { TxPhase } from './tx-progress'

const DEPLOYER = '0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8'
const STRANGER = '0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC'
const FACTORY = '0x5FbDB2315678afecb367f032d93F642f64180aa3'
const HASH: Hex = `0x${'ab'.repeat(32)}`

const form: DeployForm = {
  chainId: 31337,
  payout: DEPLOYER,
  royaltyBps: 500,
  royaltyReceiver: DEPLOYER,
  publicMaxPerWallet: 3,
  groups: [{ name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 777 }],
}

const prepared: Prepared = {
  chainId: 31337,
  factory: FACTORY,
  deployer: DEPLOYER,
  salt: `0x${'0f'.repeat(16)}${'0'.repeat(32)}`,
  codeHash: `0x${'a5'.repeat(32)}`,
  args: {
    name: 'Night Owls',
    symbol: 'OWLS',
    payout: DEPLOYER,
    baseUri: 'ipfs://start/',
    maxSupply: '777',
    groupSupplies: ['777'],
    groupPrices: ['0'],
    publicMaxPerWallet: '3',
    royaltyBps: 500,
    owner: DEPLOYER,
    royaltyReceiver: DEPLOYER,
  },
  groups: form.groups,
  encodedArgs: '0x',
  predicted: '0x0409454C375c8a6aB05383bFB6D9FcDe9ebB8F85',
  baseUri: 'ipfs://start/',
  revealsTo: null,
  fees: { mintFeeFlatWei: '0', mintFeeBps: 0, airdropFeePerTokenWei: '0' },
}

const record: DeployRecord = {
  chainId: 31337,
  contractAddress: prepared.predicted,
  txHash: HASH,
  deployer: DEPLOYER,
  deployedAt: 1_791_000_000,
  baseUri: 'ipfs://start/',
  payout: DEPLOYER,
  royaltyBps: 500,
  royaltyReceiver: DEPLOYER,
  publicMaxPerWallet: 3,
  fees: prepared.fees,
  groups: [{ onchainId: 0, name: 'Public', priceWei: '0', maxSupply: 777 }],
}

const input: DeployInput = { form, account: DEPLOYER, connectedChainId: 31337 }
const NOT_YET: ActionResult<DeployRecord> = { ok: false, error: DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED }

function fakeDeps(confirms: ActionResult<DeployRecord>[], overrides: Partial<DeployDeps> = {}) {
  let clock = 0
  const calls = {
    prepare: 0,
    switchChain: [] as number[],
    simulate: 0,
    send: 0,
    confirm: 0,
    remember: [] as PendingDeploy[],
    forget: 0,
    sleeps: [] as number[],
  }
  const deps: DeployDeps = {
    prepare: async () => {
      calls.prepare++
      return { ok: true, data: prepared }
    },
    switchChain: async (chainId) => {
      calls.switchChain.push(chainId)
    },
    simulate: async () => {
      calls.simulate++
      return async () => {
        calls.send++
        return HASH
      }
    },
    wait: async () => 'success',
    confirm: async () => {
      calls.confirm++
      return confirms.shift() ?? NOT_YET
    },
    remember: (hash, remembered) => {
      calls.remember.push({ hash, form: remembered })
    },
    forget: () => {
      calls.forget++
    },
    sleep: async (ms) => {
      calls.sleeps.push(ms)
      clock += ms
    },
    now: () => clock,
    ...overrides,
  }
  return { deps, calls }
}

async function run(deps: DeployDeps, deployInput: DeployInput = input) {
  const events: DeployEvent[] = []
  let phase: TxPhase = { step: 'idle' }
  const result = await runDeploy(deployInput, deps, (event) => {
    events.push(event)
    phase = deployReducer(phase, event)
  })
  return { result, events, last: events[events.length - 1], phase }
}

describe('runDeploy', () => {
  it('prepares, simulates, sends, waits, then polls the server every 3 seconds until it records', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([NOT_YET, NOT_YET, { ok: true, data: record }])
    const { result, events } = await run(deps)
    expect(events.map((event) => event.type)).toEqual([
      'preparing',
      'simulating',
      'wallet',
      'confirming',
      'verifying',
      'success',
    ])
    expect(calls.prepare).toBe(1)
    expect(calls.switchChain).toEqual([])
    expect(calls.sleeps).toEqual([3_000, 3_000])
    expect(calls.remember).toEqual([{ hash: HASH, form }])
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(1)
    expect(result).toEqual(record)
  })

  it('switches the wallet first when it is on another chain, and never deploys there (Review Focus 2)', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: true, data: record }])
    const { events, result } = await run(deps, { ...input, connectedChainId: 1 })
    expect(events.map((event) => event.type)).toEqual([
      'preparing',
      'switching',
      'simulating',
      'wallet',
      'confirming',
      'verifying',
      'success',
    ])
    expect(calls.switchChain).toEqual([31337])
    expect(result).toEqual(record)
  })

  it('checks a deploy already sent instead of deploying again', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: true, data: record }])
    const { events } = await run(deps, { ...input, sentHash: HASH })
    expect(calls.prepare).toBe(0)
    expect(calls.simulate).toBe(0)
    expect(events.map((event) => event.type)).toEqual(['verifying', 'success'])
  })

  it('never opens the wallet without an account, for an account that is not the sign-in wallet, or when the server refuses to prepare (Review Focus 4)', async () => {
    const noWallet = fakeDeps([])
    const first = await run(noWallet.deps, { ...input, account: undefined })
    expect(noWallet.calls.prepare).toBe(0)
    expect(first.last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'refused', title: 'Connect your wallet' } })

    const other = fakeDeps([])
    const second = await run(other.deps, { ...input, account: STRANGER })
    expect(other.calls.simulate).toBe(0)
    expect(second.last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'refused', title: 'Use your sign-in wallet' } })

    const refused = fakeDeps([], { prepare: async () => ({ ok: false, error: LINK_REQUIRED }) })
    const third = await run(refused.deps)
    expect(refused.calls.simulate).toBe(0)
    expect(third.last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'refused', message: LINK_REQUIRED } })
    expect(third.phase).toMatchObject({ step: 'failed', at: 'preparing' })

    const thrown = fakeDeps([], {
      prepare: async () => {
        throw new Error('fetch failed')
      },
    })
    const fourth = await run(thrown.deps)
    expect(fourth.last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'refused', message: THROWN } })
  })

  it('a rejected switch simulates nothing, sends nothing and remembers nothing (Review Focus 4)', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([], {
      switchChain: async () => {
        throw Object.assign(new Error('User rejected the request.'), { code: 4001 })
      },
    })
    const { last, phase } = await run(deps, { ...input, connectedChainId: 1 })
    expect(calls.simulate).toBe(0)
    expect(calls.send).toBe(0)
    expect(calls.remember).toEqual([])
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'switch', title: 'Network switch cancelled' } })
    expect(phase).toMatchObject({ step: 'failed', at: 'switching' })
  })

  it('reports a transaction that reverted on chain and forgets its hash', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([], { wait: async () => 'reverted' })
    const { last, result } = await run(deps)
    expect(result).toBeNull()
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'reverted', title: 'The deploy failed on chain' } })
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(1)
  })

  it('stops after 2 minutes of "not confirmed yet" and keeps the hash to check again', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([])
    const { last, phase } = await run(deps)
    expect(calls.confirm).toBe(41)
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', message: DEPLOY_TIMED_OUT } })
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(0)
    expect(retryHash(phase)).toBe(HASH)
  })

  it.each([NOT_THIS_DEPLOY, OTHER_CODE, OTHER_CONTRACT, DEPLOY_REVERTED, DEPLOY_USED, ALREADY_DEPLOYED])(
    'a terminal refusal (%s) is shown as it is and forgets the hash',
    async (error) => {
      const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: false, error }])
      const { last } = await run(deps, { ...input, sentHash: HASH })
      expect(last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'refused', message: error } })
      expect(calls.forget).toBe(1)
    },
  )

  it('a prepare refusal on confirm (the link lapsed meanwhile) keeps the hash and stays checkable (Review Focus 3)', async () => {
    const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: false, error: LINK_REQUIRED }])
    const { last, phase } = await run(deps)
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', message: LINK_REQUIRED } })
    expect(calls.remember).toEqual([{ hash: HASH, form }])
    expect(calls.forget).toBe(0)
    expect(retryHash(phase)).toBe(HASH)
  })

  it.each([SIGNED_OUT, THROWN, UNSAVED, CHAIN_NOT_READY, NOT_FOUND])(
    'a transient refusal (%s) keeps the hash and stays checkable',
    async (error) => {
      const { deps, calls } = fakeDeps([{ ok: false, error }])
      const { phase } = await run(deps)
      expect(phase).toMatchObject({ step: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', message: error } })
      expect(retryHash(phase)).toBe(HASH)
      expect(calls.forget).toBe(0)
    },
  )

  it('a wait that throws keeps the sent hash and offers a check; a confirm that throws is transient too', async () => {
    const waitThrows = fakeDeps([], {
      wait: async () => {
        throw new Error('timeout')
      },
    })
    const first = await run(waitThrows.deps)
    expect(first.last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed' } })
    expect(waitThrows.calls.remember).toEqual([{ hash: HASH, form }])
    expect(waitThrows.calls.forget).toBe(0)
    expect(waitThrows.calls.confirm).toBe(0)

    const confirmThrows = fakeDeps([], {
      confirm: async () => {
        throw new Error('fetch failed')
      },
    })
    const second = await run(confirmThrows.deps, { ...input, sentHash: HASH })
    expect(second.last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', message: THROWN } })
    expect(confirmThrows.calls.forget).toBe(0)
  })

  it('a simulation failure sends nothing and remembers nothing; a wallet rejection reads as rejected', async () => {
    const simulateFails = fakeDeps([], {
      simulate: async () => {
        throw Object.assign(new Error('reverted'), { shortMessage: 'Execution reverted: DeploymentFailed.' })
      },
    })
    const first = await run(simulateFails.deps)
    expect(first.last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'reverted', title: 'The deploy would fail' } })
    expect((first.last as { failure: { message: string } }).failure.message).toContain('DeploymentFailed')
    expect(simulateFails.calls.send).toBe(0)
    expect(simulateFails.calls.remember).toEqual([])

    const rejected = fakeDeps([], {
      simulate: async () => async () => {
        throw Object.assign(new Error('User rejected'), { code: 4001 })
      },
    })
    const second = await run(rejected.deps)
    expect(second.last).toMatchObject({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'rejected', title: 'Deploy cancelled in your wallet' } })
    expect(rejected.calls.remember).toEqual([])
  })
})

describe('deployReducer and retryHash', () => {
  it('fails at the step it was on and keeps the sent hash; an idle failure is at preparing', () => {
    const failure = { kind: 'unconfirmed' as const, title: 't', message: 'm' }
    const confirming = deployReducer({ step: 'idle' }, { type: 'confirming', txHash: HASH })
    const failed = deployReducer(confirming, { type: 'failed', failure })
    expect(failed).toEqual({ step: 'failed', at: 'confirming', failure, txHash: HASH })
    expect(deployReducer({ step: 'idle' }, { type: 'failed', failure })).toEqual({
      step: 'failed',
      at: 'preparing',
      failure,
    })
    expect(deployReducer({ step: 'idle' }, { type: 'switching' })).toEqual({ step: 'switching' })
    expect(retryHash(failed)).toBe(HASH)
    expect(retryHash({ step: 'failed', at: 'verifying', failure: { ...failure, kind: 'refused' }, txHash: HASH })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(retryHash({ step: 'idle' })).toBeUndefined()
  })
})

describe('the pending deploy kept in the browser', () => {
  function memoryStore(): KeyStore {
    const map = new Map<string, string>()
    return {
      getItem: (key) => map.get(key) ?? null,
      setItem: (key, value) => {
        map.set(key, value)
      },
      removeItem: (key) => {
        map.delete(key)
      },
    }
  }
  const blocked: KeyStore = {
    getItem: () => {
      throw new Error('blocked')
    },
    setItem: () => {
      throw new Error('blocked')
    },
    removeItem: () => {
      throw new Error('blocked')
    },
  }

  it('remembers the sent hash and the form per collection until they are forgotten', () => {
    const store = memoryStore()
    rememberDeploy(store, 'col-1', { hash: HASH, form })
    expect(pendingDeploy(store, 'col-1')).toEqual({ hash: HASH, form })
    expect(pendingDeploy(store, 'col-2')).toBeNull()
    expect(store.getItem('cobweb-deploy:col-1')).toContain(HASH)
    forgetDeploy(store, 'col-1')
    expect(pendingDeploy(store, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('ignores a value that is not a pending deploy and a store that throws', () => {
    const store = memoryStore()
    store.setItem('cobweb-deploy:col-1', 'nonsense')
    expect(pendingDeploy(store, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
    store.setItem('cobweb-deploy:col-1', JSON.stringify({ hash: 'not-a-hash', form }))
    expect(pendingDeploy(store, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
    store.setItem('cobweb-deploy:col-1', JSON.stringify({ hash: HASH }))
    expect(pendingDeploy(store, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
    expect(() => rememberDeploy(blocked, 'col-1', { hash: HASH, form })).not.toThrow()
    expect(pendingDeploy(blocked, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
    expect(pendingDeploy(null, 'col-1')).toBeNull()
    expect(() => forgetDeploy(null, 'col-1')).not.toThrow()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The shared classifier and the step union**

Create `apps/web/src/components/deploy/tx-errors.ts`:

```ts
import type { TxFailure } from './tx-progress'

/**
 * A wallet or chain error as the creator reads it, shared by the pay machine and the deploy machine
 * (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 7.1). The wording follows the subject: the payment
 * sentences are the ones the pay step has shown since Phase 3.
 */
export type TxSubject = 'payment' | 'deploy'
export type TxErrorStep = 'switching' | 'simulating' | 'wallet' | 'confirming'

type Words = {
  rejected: TxFailure
  balance: TxFailure
  switchRejected: string
  simulateTitle: string
  walletTitle: string
  walletTail: string
  confirmTitle: string
  confirmTail: string
}

const WORDS: Record<TxSubject, Words> = {
  payment: {
    rejected: {
      kind: 'rejected',
      title: 'Payment cancelled in your wallet',
      message: 'Nothing was sent and nothing was charged. Try again when you are ready.',
    },
    balance: {
      kind: 'balance',
      title: 'Not enough funds in this wallet',
      message:
        'The fee and network gas are more than this wallet holds on the network. Add funds, then try again.',
    },
    switchRejected:
      'Your wallet stayed on its current network, so nothing was paid. Try again and approve the switch.',
    simulateTitle: 'The payment would fail',
    walletTitle: 'The wallet did not send the payment',
    walletTail: 'Nothing was charged.',
    confirmTitle: 'The payment could not be confirmed',
    confirmTail: "Check your wallet's activity before you pay again.",
  },
  deploy: {
    rejected: {
      kind: 'rejected',
      title: 'Deploy cancelled in your wallet',
      message: 'Nothing was sent and no gas was spent. Try again when you are ready.',
    },
    balance: {
      kind: 'balance',
      title: 'Not enough funds for gas',
      message: 'This wallet cannot cover the deploy cost on this network. Add funds, then try again.',
    },
    switchRejected:
      'Your wallet stayed on its current network, so nothing was deployed. Try again and approve the switch.',
    simulateTitle: 'The deploy would fail',
    walletTitle: 'The wallet did not send the deploy',
    walletTail: 'No gas was spent.',
    confirmTitle: 'The deploy could not be confirmed',
    confirmTail: "Check your wallet's activity before you deploy again.",
  },
}

function causes(error: unknown): unknown[] {
  const list: unknown[] = []
  let current: unknown = error
  for (let depth = 0; current && depth < 10; depth++) {
    list.push(current)
    current = (current as { cause?: unknown }).cause
  }
  return list
}

function shortMessageOf(error: unknown): string {
  return (
    (error as { shortMessage?: string }).shortMessage ??
    (error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error.')
  )
}

/** A wallet or chain error as the creator reads it, for the step it happened at. */
export function classifyError(error: unknown, at: TxErrorStep, subject: TxSubject = 'payment'): TxFailure {
  const words = WORDS[subject]
  const chain = causes(error)
  const rejected = chain.some(
    (entry) =>
      (entry as { name?: string }).name === 'UserRejectedRequestError' ||
      (entry as { code?: unknown }).code === 4001,
  )
  if (at === 'switching') {
    return rejected
      ? { kind: 'switch', title: 'Network switch cancelled', message: words.switchRejected }
      : {
          kind: 'switch',
          title: 'The network switch failed',
          message: `${shortMessageOf(error)} Switch your wallet by hand, then try again.`,
        }
  }
  if (rejected) return words.rejected
  const text = chain.map((entry) => (entry instanceof Error ? entry.message : String(entry))).join(' ')
  if (/insufficient funds/i.test(text)) return words.balance
  const short = shortMessageOf(error)
  if (at === 'simulating')
    return {
      kind: 'reverted',
      title: words.simulateTitle,
      message: `The check against the factory failed: ${short} Nothing was sent.`,
    }
  if (at === 'wallet') return { kind: 'rejected', title: words.walletTitle, message: `${short} ${words.walletTail}` }
  return { kind: 'reverted', title: words.confirmTitle, message: `${short} ${words.confirmTail}` }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The deploy machine**

Create `apps/web/src/components/deploy/deploy-machine.ts`:

```ts
import type { Hex } from 'viem'
import {
  ALREADY_DEPLOYED,
  DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED,
  DEPLOY_REVERTED,
  DEPLOY_TIMED_OUT,
  DEPLOY_USED,
  NOT_THIS_DEPLOY,
  OTHER_CODE,
  OTHER_CONTRACT,
  THROWN,
  type ActionResult,
} from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { DeployForm, DeployRecord, Prepared } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'
import { classifyError } from './tx-errors'
import type { TxFailure, TxPhase } from './tx-progress'

/**
 * The deploy as a pure machine (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 7.1), shaped like the pay
 * machine so every path is tested in Node: the server prepares the constructor and the prediction, the
 * wallet switches chain when it is elsewhere, simulates, signs, the receipt lands, then the server's
 * confirmation is polled every 3 seconds for up to 2 minutes. `DeployFlow` wires the actions in.
 * CLAUDE.md rule 2 holds by construction: `simulate` returns the only way to send, and it sends the
 * simulated request.
 */
export const CONFIRM_INTERVAL_MS = 3_000
export const CONFIRM_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000

export type DeployEvent =
  | { type: 'set'; phase: TxPhase }
  | { type: 'preparing' }
  | { type: 'switching' }
  | { type: 'simulating' }
  | { type: 'wallet' }
  | { type: 'confirming'; txHash: Hex }
  | { type: 'verifying'; txHash: Hex }
  | { type: 'success'; txHash: Hex }
  | { type: 'failed'; failure: TxFailure }

export function deployReducer(phase: TxPhase, event: DeployEvent): TxPhase {
  switch (event.type) {
    case 'set':
      return event.phase
    case 'preparing':
      return { step: 'preparing' }
    case 'switching':
      return { step: 'switching' }
    case 'simulating':
      return { step: 'simulating' }
    case 'wallet':
      return { step: 'wallet' }
    case 'confirming':
      return { step: 'confirming', txHash: event.txHash }
    case 'verifying':
      return { step: 'verifying', txHash: event.txHash }
    case 'success':
      return { step: 'success', txHash: event.txHash }
    case 'failed': {
      if (phase.step === 'idle' || phase.step === 'success')
        return { step: 'failed', at: 'preparing', failure: event.failure }
      const at = phase.step === 'failed' ? phase.at : phase.step
      return phase.txHash
        ? { step: 'failed', at, failure: event.failure, txHash: phase.txHash }
        : { step: 'failed', at, failure: event.failure }
    }
  }
}

export type DeployInput = {
  form: DeployForm
  /** The connected account, if any. It must be the sign-in wallet: the server prepares for that deployer. */
  account: string | undefined
  connectedChainId: number | undefined
  /** A transaction already sent for this collection: check it instead of deploying again. */
  sentHash?: Hex
}

export type DeployDeps = {
  prepare(form: DeployForm): Promise<ActionResult<Prepared>>
  switchChain(chainId: number): Promise<void>
  /** Simulates `deployCollection` with the prepared bytes; resolves with the function that sends the simulated request, unchanged. */
  simulate(prepared: Prepared): Promise<() => Promise<Hex>>
  wait(hash: Hex): Promise<'success' | 'reverted'>
  confirm(hash: Hex, form: DeployForm): Promise<ActionResult<DeployRecord>>
  remember(hash: Hex, form: DeployForm): void
  forget(): void
  sleep(ms: number): Promise<void>
  now(): number
}

const NO_WALLET: TxFailure = {
  kind: 'refused',
  title: 'Connect your wallet',
  message: 'Connect your wallet to deploy. Nothing was sent.',
}
const PREPARE_FAILED = 'Cobweb could not prepare the deploy'
const REVERTED_ON_CHAIN: TxFailure = {
  kind: 'reverted',
  title: 'The deploy failed on chain',
  message: 'The transaction was included but reverted, so no contract was created. Try again.',
}

/**
 * Asks the server to confirm a deployment every 3 seconds while it answers "not confirmed yet", for up
 * to 2 minutes. A confirm that throws is a transient failure.
 */
export async function verifyDeploy(
  hash: Hex,
  form: DeployForm,
  deps: Pick<DeployDeps, 'confirm' | 'sleep' | 'now'>,
): Promise<{ record: DeployRecord } | { error: string }> {
  const started = deps.now()
  for (;;) {
    let result: ActionResult<DeployRecord>
    try {
      result = await deps.confirm(hash, form)
    } catch {
      return { error: THROWN }
    }
    if (result.ok) return { record: result.data }
    if (result.error !== DEPLOY_NOT_CONFIRMED) return { error: result.error }
    if (deps.now() - started + CONFIRM_INTERVAL_MS > CONFIRM_TIMEOUT_MS) return { error: DEPLOY_TIMED_OUT }
    await deps.sleep(CONFIRM_INTERVAL_MS)
  }
}

/**
 * Whether the server's answer ends this deploy attempt for good, so this browser forgets the hash:
 * the receipt proved something else, or the collection is already deployed. Any other refusal (the
 * link lapsed meanwhile, a session that ended, a thrown action, a chain not ready) says nothing about
 * the transaction: the hash stays and "Check again" is offered once the cause is fixed.
 */
export function isTerminalDeployAnswer(error: string): boolean {
  return (
    error === NOT_THIS_DEPLOY ||
    error === OTHER_CODE ||
    error === OTHER_CONTRACT ||
    error === DEPLOY_REVERTED ||
    error === DEPLOY_USED ||
    error === ALREADY_DEPLOYED
  )
}

/** Deploys the contract, or checks a deploy already sent, reporting each step through `dispatch`. */
export async function runDeploy(
  input: DeployInput,
  deps: DeployDeps,
  dispatch: (event: DeployEvent) => void,
): Promise<DeployRecord | null> {
  let hash = input.sentHash
  if (!hash) {
    if (!input.account) {
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: NO_WALLET })
      return null
    }
    dispatch({ type: 'preparing' })
    let prepared: Prepared
    try {
      const result = await deps.prepare(input.form)
      if (!result.ok) {
        dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'refused', title: PREPARE_FAILED, message: result.error } })
        return null
      }
      prepared = result.data
    } catch {
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: { kind: 'refused', title: PREPARE_FAILED, message: THROWN } })
      return null
    }
    // The server prepared for the sign-in wallet; a deploy from any other account lands at another
    // address and is never recorded, so it must not reach the wallet.
    if (input.account.toLowerCase() !== prepared.deployer.toLowerCase()) {
      dispatch({
        type: 'failed',
        failure: {
          kind: 'refused',
          title: 'Use your sign-in wallet',
          message: `Only a deploy from ${prepared.deployer} is recorded. Switch to that account in your wallet, then try again.`,
        },
      })
      return null
    }
    if (input.connectedChainId !== prepared.chainId) {
      dispatch({ type: 'switching' })
      try {
        await deps.switchChain(prepared.chainId)
      } catch (error) {
        dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: classifyError(error, 'switching', 'deploy') })
        return null
      }
    }
    dispatch({ type: 'simulating' })
    let send: () => Promise<Hex>
    try {
      send = await deps.simulate(prepared)
    } catch (error) {
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: classifyError(error, 'simulating', 'deploy') })
      return null
    }
    dispatch({ type: 'wallet' })
    try {
      hash = await send()
    } catch (error) {
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: classifyError(error, 'wallet', 'deploy') })
      return null
    }
    deps.remember(hash, input.form)
    dispatch({ type: 'confirming', txHash: hash })
    let status: 'success' | 'reverted'
    try {
      status = await deps.wait(hash)
    } catch (error) {
      // The receipt could not be read: the transaction may still land, so keep the hash and offer a check.
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: { ...classifyError(error, 'confirming', 'deploy'), kind: 'unconfirmed' } })
      return null
    }
    if (status === 'reverted') {
      deps.forget()
      dispatch({ type: 'failed', failure: REVERTED_ON_CHAIN })
      return null
    }
  }
  dispatch({ type: 'verifying', txHash: hash })
  const verified = await verifyDeploy(hash, input.form, deps)
  if ('record' in verified) {
    deps.forget()
    dispatch({ type: 'success', txHash: hash })
    return verified.record
  }
  if (!isTerminalDeployAnswer(verified.error)) {
    // Timed out or a transient refusal: the contract may still be recorded, so the hash stays.
    dispatch({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: { kind: 'unconfirmed', title: 'Deploy not confirmed yet', message: verified.error },
    })
  } else {
    deps.forget()
    dispatch({
      type: 'failed',
      failure: { kind: 'refused', title: 'Cobweb could not record this deploy', message: verified.error },
    })
  }
  return null
}

/** The hash "Check again" checks instead of deploying: a deploy sent but not confirmed in time. */
export function retryHash(phase: TxPhase): Hex | undefined {
  return phase.step === 'failed' && phase.failure.kind === 'unconfirmed' ? phase.txHash : undefined
}

export type KeyStore = Pick<Storage, 'getItem' | 'setItem' | 'removeItem'>
/** What the browser keeps until the server records the contract: the sent hash and the form it was sent with. */
export type PendingDeploy = { hash: Hex; form: DeployForm }

const keyFor = (collectionId: string) => `cobweb-deploy:${collectionId}`
const HASH_RE = /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/

/** The browser's localStorage, or null where it is blocked or absent. */
export function browserStore(): KeyStore | null {
  try {
    return typeof window === 'undefined' ? null : window.localStorage
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

export function rememberDeploy(store: KeyStore | null, collectionId: string, pending: PendingDeploy): void {
  try {
    store?.setItem(keyFor(collectionId), JSON.stringify(pending))
  } catch {
    // Storage blocked: the deploy is still checked in this visit.
  }
}

/** A deploy this browser sent for the collection and the server has not recorded yet, or null. */
export function pendingDeploy(store: KeyStore | null, collectionId: string): PendingDeploy | null {
  try {
    const value = store?.getItem(keyFor(collectionId)) ?? null
    if (!value) return null
    const parsed = JSON.parse(value) as { hash?: unknown; form?: unknown }
    if (typeof parsed.hash !== 'string' || !HASH_RE.test(parsed.hash)) return null
    const form = parsed.form as DeployForm | undefined
    if (!form || typeof form !== 'object' || typeof form.chainId !== 'number' || !Array.isArray(form.groups))
      return null
    return { hash: parsed.hash as Hex, form }
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

export function forgetDeploy(store: KeyStore | null, collectionId: string): void {
  try {
    store?.removeItem(keyFor(collectionId))
  } catch {
    // Storage blocked: nothing was kept.
  }
}
```
