- [ ] **Step 1: The real actions**

Create `apps/web/src/components/deploy/deploy-actions.ts`:

```ts
import { factoryAbi } from '@cobweb/shared'
import { COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE } from '@cobweb/shared/collection'
import type { Address, Hex } from 'viem'
import type { Config } from 'wagmi'
import {
  getPublicClient,
  readContract,
  simulateContract,
  switchChain,
  waitForTransactionReceipt,
  writeContract,
} from 'wagmi/actions'
import { confirmDeploy, prepareDeploy } from '@/app/actions/deploy'
import type { ActionResult } from '@/lib/art/result'
import type { FeeSnapshot } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import type { DeployForm, DeployRecord, Prepared } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'

/**
 * What the Deploy screen does, in one shape for both modes (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md,
 * sections 7.2 and 8): `realDeployActions` runs the server actions and wagmi, `sampleDeployActions`
 * (deploy-sim.ts) simulates in the browser. The deploy machine's `DeployDeps` are built from this plus
 * the browser store and the timers (deploy-flow.tsx).
 */
export type DeployActions = {
  prepare(form: DeployForm): Promise<ActionResult<Prepared>>
  /** The factory's `collectionFees()` on a chain; throws when it cannot be read. */
  fees(chainId: number, factory: Address | null): Promise<FeeSnapshot>
  /** The deploy's cost in wei as text (the gas of the prepared call times the gas price), or null when it cannot be estimated. */
  estimateCost(prepared: Prepared): Promise<string | null>
  switchChain(chainId: number): Promise<void>
  /** Simulates `deployCollection` with the prepared bytes; resolves with the function that sends the simulated request, unchanged. */
  simulate(prepared: Prepared): Promise<() => Promise<Hex>>
  wait(hash: Hex, chainId: number): Promise<'success' | 'reverted'>
  confirm(hash: Hex, form: DeployForm): Promise<ActionResult<DeployRecord>>
}

/** The real actions: the server prepares and confirms; wagmi reads, switches, simulates, sends and waits. */
export function realDeployActions(
  config: Config,
  collectionId: string,
  account: () => Address | undefined,
): DeployActions {
  const callArgs = (prepared: Prepared) =>
    [COLLECTION_CREATION_CODE, prepared.encodedArgs, prepared.salt] as const
  return {
    prepare: (form) => prepareDeploy(collectionId, form),
    fees: async (chainId, factory) => {
      if (!factory) throw new Error(`The factory on chain ${chainId} is not configured.`)
      const [flat, bps, airdrop] = await readContract(config, {
        address: factory,
        abi: factoryAbi,
        functionName: 'collectionFees',
        chainId,
      })
      return { mintFeeFlatWei: flat.toString(), mintFeeBps: Number(bps), airdropFeePerTokenWei: airdrop.toString() }
    },
    estimateCost: async (prepared) => {
      const client = getPublicClient(config, { chainId: prepared.chainId })
      const from = account()
      if (!client || !from) return null
      try {
        const [gas, price] = await Promise.all([
          client.estimateContractGas({
            address: prepared.factory,
            abi: factoryAbi,
            functionName: 'deployCollection',
            args: callArgs(prepared),
            account: from,
          }),
          client.getGasPrice(),
        ])
        return (gas * price).toString()
      } catch {
        // The estimate is a courtesy: the wallet shows the real gas before signing.
        return null
      }
    },
    switchChain: async (chainId) => {
      await switchChain(config, { chainId })
    },
    simulate: async (prepared) => {
      const simulated = await simulateContract(config, {
        address: prepared.factory,
        abi: factoryAbi,
        functionName: 'deployCollection',
        args: callArgs(prepared),
        chainId: prepared.chainId,
        account: account(),
      })
      // CLAUDE.md rule 2: the wallet gets exactly the request the simulation passed.
      return () => writeContract(config, simulated.request)
    },
    wait: async (hash, chainId) => (await waitForTransactionReceipt(config, { hash, chainId })).status,
    confirm: (hash, form) => confirmDeploy(collectionId, hash, form),
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Sample mode on the same surface**

Replace `apps/web/src/components/deploy/deploy-sim.ts` with:

```ts
/**
 * Sample mode only (`NEXT_PUBLIC_DEMO_DATA=1`): the deploy actions simulated in client state, so
 * every state can be reviewed without a wallet, a chain or a database (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md,
 * section 8). Real mode uses `realDeployActions` (deploy-actions.ts). Failures are driven by
 * `?outcome=` and fire once, on the first run of the step they belong to, so a retry then goes through.
 */
import { collectionSalt } from '@cobweb/shared'
import type { Address, Hex } from 'viem'
import { useAccount } from 'wagmi'
import { DEMO_DEPLOY_COST_ESTIMATE, DEMO_DEPLOYMENT, DEMO_FACTORY_FEES } from '@/lib/demo'
import { sampleAddress, sampleTxHash } from '@/lib/demo/helpers'
import type { DeployForm, DeployRecord, Prepared } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'
import type { DeployActions } from './deploy-actions'

export type DeployOutcome = 'rejected' | 'reverted' | 'balance' | 'switch-rejected' | 'fees-error'

export const DEPLOY_OUTCOMES: DeployOutcome[] = [
  'rejected',
  'reverted',
  'balance',
  'switch-rejected',
  'fees-error',
]

/** The chain a wallet reports in sample mode when none is connected, so a switch shows when another is picked. */
const SAMPLE_WALLET_CHAIN = 1
const SAMPLE_FACTORY: Address = '0x5FbDB2315678afecb367f032d93F642f64180aa3'
const SAMPLE_CODE_HASH: Hex = `0x${'7'.repeat(64)}`

const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))

/**
 * The connected wallet and its chain, or the fallback address (the session's wallet in real mode, the
 * sample creator in sample mode) and, in sample mode only, Ethereum as the chain.
 */
export function useCreatorWallet(
  fallback: Address,
  sample: boolean,
): { address: Address; connected: boolean; chainId: number | undefined } {
  const account = useAccount()
  return {
    address: account.address ?? fallback,
    connected: account.address !== undefined,
    chainId: account.chainId ?? (sample ? SAMPLE_WALLET_CHAIN : undefined),
  }
}

/** The address the sample "predicts" for these settings: a stable hash of them. */
export function sampleAddressFor(collectionId: string, form: DeployForm): Address {
  const text = `${collectionId}|${JSON.stringify(form)}`
  let hash = 2166136261
  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index++) {
    hash ^= text.charCodeAt(index)
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619)
  }
  return sampleAddress(hash >>> 0)
}

export type SampleDeployInput = {
  collectionId: string
  collection: { name: string; symbol: string; maxSupply: number }
  /** The wallet the sample deploys from. */
  wallet: Address
  baseUri: string
  revealsTo: string | null
  outcome?: DeployOutcome
}

function samplePrepared(input: SampleDeployInput, form: DeployForm): Prepared {
  return {
    chainId: form.chainId,
    factory: SAMPLE_FACTORY,
    deployer: input.wallet,
    salt: collectionSalt(input.collectionId),
    codeHash: SAMPLE_CODE_HASH,
    args: {
      name: input.collection.name,
      symbol: input.collection.symbol,
      payout: form.payout,
      baseUri: input.baseUri,
      maxSupply: String(input.collection.maxSupply),
      groupSupplies: form.groups.map((group) => String(group.maxSupply)),
      groupPrices: form.groups.map((group) => group.priceWei),
      publicMaxPerWallet: String(form.publicMaxPerWallet),
      royaltyBps: form.royaltyBps,
      owner: input.wallet,
      royaltyReceiver: form.royaltyReceiver,
    },
    groups: form.groups,
    encodedArgs: '0x',
    predicted: sampleAddressFor(input.collectionId, form),
    baseUri: input.baseUri,
    revealsTo: input.revealsTo,
    fees: DEMO_FACTORY_FEES[form.chainId] ?? DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.fees,
  }
}

/** The record of a sample deploy that just landed: the creator's own settings at the sample's prediction. */
function recordOf(prepared: Prepared, hash: Hex): DeployRecord {
  return {
    chainId: prepared.chainId,
    contractAddress: prepared.predicted,
    txHash: hash,
    deployer: prepared.deployer,
    deployedAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
    baseUri: prepared.baseUri,
    payout: prepared.args.payout,
    royaltyBps: prepared.args.royaltyBps,
    royaltyReceiver: prepared.args.royaltyReceiver,
    publicMaxPerWallet: Number(prepared.args.publicMaxPerWallet),
    fees: prepared.fees,
    groups: prepared.groups.map((group, index) => ({
      onchainId: index,
      name: group.name,
      priceWei: group.priceWei,
      maxSupply: group.maxSupply,
    })),
  }
}

const rejection = () => Object.assign(new Error('User rejected the request.'), { code: 4001 })

/** The screen's actions in sample mode: they wait like the real ones and write nothing. */
export function sampleDeployActions(input: SampleDeployInput): DeployActions {
  // The outcome fires once, on the first run of its step: a retry then goes through.
  let fired = false
  const failsNow = (kind: DeployOutcome) => {
    if (input.outcome !== kind || fired) return false
    fired = true
    return true
  }
  return {
    prepare: async (form) => {
      await sleep(300)
      return { ok: true, data: samplePrepared(input, form) }
    },
    fees: async (chainId) => {
      await sleep(550)
      if (failsNow('fees-error')) throw new Error('The factory did not answer.')
      const fees = DEMO_FACTORY_FEES[chainId]
      if (!fees) throw new Error(`No sample fees for chain ${chainId}.`)
      return fees
    },
    estimateCost: async (prepared) => DEMO_DEPLOY_COST_ESTIMATE[prepared.chainId] ?? null,
    switchChain: async () => {
      await sleep(1200)
      if (failsNow('switch-rejected')) throw rejection()
    },
    simulate: async () => {
      await sleep(1000)
      if (failsNow('balance')) throw new Error('insufficient funds for gas * price + value')
      if (failsNow('reverted'))
        throw Object.assign(new Error('reverted'), { shortMessage: 'Execution reverted: DeploymentFailed.' })
      return async () => {
        await sleep(1700)
        if (failsNow('rejected')) throw rejection()
        return sampleTxHash(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) % 100_000)
      }
    },
    wait: async () => {
      await sleep(2200)
      return 'success'
    },
    confirm: async (hash, form) => {
      await sleep(800)
      return { ok: true, data: recordOf(samplePrepared(input, form), hash) }
    },
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The summary**

Replace `apps/web/src/components/deploy/deploy-summary.tsx` with:

```tsx
'use client'

import { sampleSupply, DEMO_COLLECTION } from '@/lib/demo'
import { RotateCwIcon, TriangleAlertIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@cobweb/ui/components/alert'
import { Artwork } from '@cobweb/ui/components/artwork'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import { Panel } from '@cobweb/ui/components/panel'
import { Skeleton } from '@cobweb/ui/components/skeleton'
import { ChainName } from '@/components/chains/chain-icon'
import { slugState } from '@/lib/collections'
import type { Tables } from '@/lib/database.types'
import { formatBps, formatNative, shortAddress } from '@/lib/demo/helpers'
import type { Address, FeeSnapshot, SupportedChain } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import { CopyValue, SummaryRow } from './copy-value'

/** The factory's fees as read in the browser; the server's snapshot stays shown while a new read runs. */
export type FeesState = { status: 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'; data: FeeSnapshot | null }

/** The deploy cost: the gas of the prepared call times the gas price, when it can be estimated. */
export type CostState =
  | { kind: 'none' }
  | { kind: 'no-wallet' }
  | { kind: 'other-chain'; chainName: string }
  | { kind: 'loading' }
  | { kind: 'ready'; wei: string }
  | { kind: 'unknown' }

export type SummaryProps = {
  collection: Tables<'collections'>
  chain: SupportedChain | null
  fees: FeesState
  onRetryFees: () => void
  cost: CostState
  /** The server's prediction for the current settings; null while the settings are incomplete. */
  predicted: Address | null
  /** A prediction is on its way: the settings changed less than 400 ms ago, or the server is answering. */
  predicting: boolean
  payout: Address | null
  royaltyBps: number | null
  royaltyReceiver: Address | null
  /** The base URI the contract starts on, or null before any finished render. */
  startsOn: string | null
  /** The contract starts on a placeholder, so there is something to reveal to. */
  startsOnPlaceholder: boolean
  /** The real set's base URI, or null while it is not rendered. */
  revealsTo: string | null
  artSrc: string | null
}

/** What a mint costs collectors on top of the price, for a chain's fee snapshot. */
export function mintFeeText(fees: FeeSnapshot, symbol: string): string {
  return `${formatNative(fees.mintFeeFlatWei, symbol, 8)} + ${formatBps(fees.mintFeeBps)} of price`
}

/** `ipfs://bafybeihk…7lly/`: the first ten characters after the scheme and the last six. */
function shortUri(uri: string): string {
  return uri.replace(/^(ipfs:\/\/.{10}).*(.{6})$/, '$1…$2')
}

function costText(cost: CostState, symbol: string): string {
  switch (cost.kind) {
    case 'ready':
      return `about ${formatNative(cost.wei, symbol, 6)}`
    case 'loading':
      return 'Estimating'
    case 'other-chain':
      return `Could not be estimated while your wallet is on another network. It switches to ${cost.chainName} when you deploy.`
    case 'no-wallet':
      return 'Connect your wallet to estimate it.'
    case 'unknown':
      return 'Could not be estimated'
    case 'none':
      return 'Fill in the settings'
  }
}

/** The live summary beside the form: what will be deployed, where, and what it costs. */
export function DeploySummary(props: SummaryProps) {
  const { collection, chain, fees, cost, predicted, predicting, artSrc } = props
  const symbol = chain?.nativeSymbol ?? ''

  return (
    <Panel title="Summary" bodyClassName="p-0">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 border-b p-5">
        <Artwork
          src={artSrc}
          alt={`${collection.name} sample token`}
          seed={collection.id}
          className="border-border-strong size-16 shrink-0 rounded-lg border"
        />
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="truncate font-semibold">{collection.name}</p>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">
            <span className="numeric text-foreground/85">{collection.symbol}</span> ·{' '}
            <span className="numeric">
              {sampleSupply(collection.max_supply, DEMO_COLLECTION.maxSupply).toLocaleString('en-US')}
            </span>{' '}
            tokens · {chain ? <ChainName chain={chain} iconClassName="size-3.5" /> : 'No chain yet'}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <dl className="divide-y px-5">
        <SummaryRow label="Contract address">
          {predicted ? (
            <CopyValue value={predicted} label="Contract address" display={shortAddress(predicted, 6)} />
          ) : (
            <span className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">
              {predicting ? 'Predicting' : 'Fill in the settings'}
            </span>
          )}
        </SummaryRow>
        <SummaryRow label="Payout">
          {props.payout ? (
            <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]" title={props.payout}>
              {shortAddress(props.payout)}
            </span>
          ) : (
            <Missing />
          )}
        </SummaryRow>
        <SummaryRow label="Royalty">
          {props.royaltyBps !== null && props.royaltyReceiver ? (
            <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]">
              {formatBps(props.royaltyBps)} to {shortAddress(props.royaltyReceiver)}
            </span>
          ) : (
            <Missing />
          )}
        </SummaryRow>
        <SummaryRow label="Mint page link">
          <MintPageAddress collection={collection} />
        </SummaryRow>
        <SummaryRow label="Starts on">
          {props.startsOn ? (
            <span className="numeric truncate text-[0.8125rem]" title={props.startsOn}>
              {shortUri(props.startsOn)}
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-destructive-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">Not rendered yet</span>
          )}
        </SummaryRow>
        {props.startsOnPlaceholder && (
          <SummaryRow label="Reveals to">
            {props.revealsTo ? (
              <span className="numeric truncate text-[0.8125rem]" title={props.revealsTo}>
                {shortUri(props.revealsTo)}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">Not rendered yet</span>
            )}
          </SummaryRow>
        )}
      </dl>
      {predicted && (
        <p className="text-muted-foreground border-t px-5 py-3 text-xs leading-snug">
          Predicted by Cobweb from your settings. The address is the same on every chain, and it changes if you
          change a setting above.
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="bg-surface-2/40 border-t px-5 pt-4 pb-5">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-medium">Platform fees</h3>
        {!chain ? (
          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2 text-[0.8125rem]">Choose a chain to see its fees.</p>
        ) : fees.data ? (
          <>
            <dl className="mt-1 divide-y">
              <SummaryRow label="Per token minted">
                <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]">{mintFeeText(fees.data, symbol)}</span>
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Per token airdropped">
                <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]">
                  {formatNative(fees.data.airdropFeePerTokenWei, symbol, 8)}
                </span>
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Deploy cost">
                <span className={cost.kind === 'ready' ? 'numeric text-[0.8125rem]' : 'text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]'}>
                  {costText(cost, symbol)}
                </span>
              </SummaryRow>
            </dl>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2 text-xs leading-snug">
              Collectors pay the mint fee on top of your price. The contract copies these from the Cobweb
              factory when it is created; only Cobweb can change them afterwards. Deploy cost is network gas,
              paid by you.
            </p>
          </>
        ) : fees.status === 'error' ? (
          <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-3">
            <TriangleAlertIcon aria-hidden="true" />
            <AlertTitle>Could not read the fees</AlertTitle>
            <AlertDescription>
              <p>The factory on {chain.name} did not answer. Deploy stays off until the fees are known.</p>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="xs" onClick={props.onRetryFees} className="mt-1">
                <RotateCwIcon />
                Try again
              </Button>
            </AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-3 space-y-2.5" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Reading fees from the factory">
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full rounded-sm" />
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-4/5 rounded-sm" />
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-3/5 rounded-sm" />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Panel>
  )
}

function MintPageAddress({ collection }: { collection: Tables<'collections'> }) {
  const state = slugState(collection)
  if (state.kind === 'reserved' || state.kind === 'permanent')
    return (
      <span className="numeric truncate text-[0.8125rem]" title={`cobweb.app/${state.slug}`}>
        cobweb.app/{state.slug}
      </span>
    )
  return (
    <span className="text-destructive-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">
      {state.kind === 'lapsed' ? 'Lapsed. Reserve it again' : 'Reserve one to deploy'}
    </span>
  )
}

function Missing() {
  return <span className="text-destructive-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">Needs a valid address</span>
}
```

```tsx
'use client'

import { ArrowRightIcon, CheckIcon, ImageIcon, LayersIcon, ListChecksIcon, Share2Icon, ShieldCheckIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from 'motion/react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { Artwork } from '@cobweb/ui/components/artwork'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import { Stagger, StaggerItem } from '@cobweb/ui/components/motion'
import { Panel } from '@cobweb/ui/components/panel'
import { EASE_OUT, transition } from '@cobweb/ui/lib/motion'
import { ChainIcon, ChainName } from '@/components/chains/chain-icon'
import { slugState } from '@/lib/collections'
import type { Tables } from '@/lib/database.types'
import { explorerAddressUrl, explorerTxUrl, formatBps, formatNative, formatUtc, shortAddress } from '@/lib/demo/helpers'
import type { SupportedChain } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import type { DeployRecord } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'
import { useArtDeferred } from './art-deferred'
import { CopyValue, SummaryRow } from './copy-value'
import { mintFeeText } from './deploy-summary'

/** The deployed view's primary action, for the footer: Manage drop, where the phases are opened. */
export function SetUpDropButton({ collectionId }: { collectionId: string }) {
  return (
    <Button asChild>
      <Link href={`/collections/${collectionId}/manage`}>
        Set up your drop
        <ArrowRightIcon aria-hidden="true" />
      </Link>
    </Button>
  )
}

/** The deployed contract as the server recorded it: where it is, the receipt, and what to do next in Manage drop. */
export function DeployedView({
  collection,
  record,
  chain,
  artSrc,
  focusOnMount = false,
}: {
  collection: Tables<'collections'>
  record: DeployRecord
  chain: SupportedChain
  artSrc: string | null
  /** Move focus to the heading, after a deploy that just finished on this screen. */
  focusOnMount?: boolean
}) {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion()
  const headingRef = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusOnMount) headingRef.current?.focus()
  }, [focusOnMount])
  const artDeferred = useArtDeferred()
  const manage = `/collections/${collection.id}/manage`
  // A deploy makes a live reservation permanent; one that lapsed first was released.
  const state = slugState(collection)
  const slug = state.kind === 'permanent' || state.kind === 'reserved' ? state.slug : null
  // A chain with no explorer (Anvil) has no links to show.
  const explorerName = chain.explorerUrl ? new URL(chain.explorerUrl).hostname.replace(/^www\./, '') : null
  const nextSteps = [
    // Deferred art (MASTER_PLAN, Flow: pre-reveal): the contract is on the placeholder until the real set is revealed.
    ...(artDeferred
      ? [
          {
            href: `/collections/${collection.id}/art/generate`,
            icon: <ImageIcon />,
            title: 'Finish the art, then reveal',
            text: 'Art stays open. Lock and upload the real set, then reveal it from Settings.',
          },
        ]
      : []),
    {
      href: `${manage}/phases`,
      icon: <LayersIcon />,
      title: 'Open the phases',
      text: 'Every phase starts closed. Open the public sale, or schedule it, in Phases.',
    },
    {
      href: `${manage}/whitelist`,
      icon: <ListChecksIcon />,
      title: 'Upload the whitelist',
      text: 'Add wallets and their quotas to each whitelist phase.',
    },
    {
      href: `${manage}/mint-page`,
      icon: <Share2Icon />,
      title: 'Prepare the mint page',
      text: slug
        ? 'Add a banner and links. The public page opens with Phase 6.'
        : 'Choose its link, add a banner and links. The public page opens with Phase 6.',
    },
  ]

  return (
    <div className="space-y-10">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div className="relative shrink-0">
          <motion.div
            initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, scale: 0.92, y: 6 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={transition.slow}
          >
            <Artwork
              src={artSrc}
              alt={`${collection.name} sample token`}
              seed={collection.id}
              className="border-border-strong shadow-lifted size-24 rounded-xl border"
            />
          </motion.div>
          {/* The seal lands once, with a single ring that widens and fades. */}
          <motion.span
            aria-hidden="true"
            initial={reduce ? false : { scale: 0.4, opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
            transition={{ ...transition.spring, delay: reduce ? 0 : 0.25 }}
            className="bg-success text-background ring-card absolute -right-2 -bottom-2 flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full ring-4"
          >
            <CheckIcon className="size-4" strokeWidth={3} />
          </motion.span>
          {!reduce && (
            <motion.span
              aria-hidden="true"
              initial={{ scale: 0.6, opacity: 0.7 }}
              animate={{ scale: 2.2, opacity: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.9, ease: EASE_OUT, delay: 0.3 }}
              className="border-success absolute -right-2 -bottom-2 size-8 rounded-full border-2"
            />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1.5">
          <h3 ref={headingRef} className="text-title outline-none" tabIndex={-1}>
            {collection.name} is deployed on{' '}
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 align-bottom">
              <ChainIcon chainId={chain.id} className="text-muted-foreground" />
              {chain.name}
            </span>
          </h3>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.9375rem] leading-relaxed">
            The contract is deployed and every phase starts closed. Nothing can be minted until you open a
            phase.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.2fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <div className="space-y-5">
          <Panel title="Contract" bodyClassName="px-6 py-2">
            <dl className="divide-y">
              <SummaryRow label="Chain">
                <ChainName chain={chain} className="text-[0.8125rem]" />
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Address">
                <CopyValue
                  value={record.contractAddress}
                  label="Contract address"
                  display={shortAddress(record.contractAddress, 8)}
                  href={explorerName ? explorerAddressUrl(chain, record.contractAddress) : undefined}
                  linkLabel={explorerName ? `View on ${explorerName}` : undefined}
                />
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Transaction">
                <CopyValue
                  value={record.txHash}
                  label="Transaction hash"
                  display={shortAddress(record.txHash, 8)}
                  href={explorerName ? explorerTxUrl(chain, record.txHash) : undefined}
                  linkLabel={explorerName ? `View on ${explorerName}` : undefined}
                />
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Deployed">
                <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]">{formatUtc(record.deployedAt)}</span>
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Deployed by">
                <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]" title={record.deployer}>
                  {shortAddress(record.deployer)}
                </span>
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Starts on">
                <span className="numeric truncate text-[0.8125rem]" title={record.baseUri}>
                  {record.baseUri.replace(/^(ipfs:\/\/.{10}).*(.{6})$/, '$1…$2')}
                </span>
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Mint page link">
                {slug ? (
                  <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-col items-end">
                    <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]">cobweb.app/{slug}</span>
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">Opens with the public mint page in Phase 6.</span>
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">None yet. Set it in Manage drop.</span>
                )}
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Payout">
                <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]" title={record.payout}>
                  {shortAddress(record.payout)}
                </span>
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Royalty">
                <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]">
                  {formatBps(record.royaltyBps)} to {shortAddress(record.royaltyReceiver)}
                </span>
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Public limit">
                <span>
                  <span className="numeric">{record.publicMaxPerWallet}</span> per wallet
                </span>
              </SummaryRow>
              <SummaryRow label="Mint fee">
                <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]">{mintFeeText(record.fees, chain.nativeSymbol)}</span>
              </SummaryRow>
            </dl>
            <div className="bg-surface-2/40 -mx-6 mt-2 -mb-2 flex gap-3 rounded-b-xl border-t px-6 py-4">
              <ShieldCheckIcon aria-hidden="true" className="text-muted-foreground mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" />
              <div className="space-y-1 text-[0.8125rem] leading-snug">
                <p className="font-medium">
                  Royalties <span className="numeric">{formatBps(record.royaltyBps)}</span>, enforced on
                  secondary sales through the transfer validator
                </p>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground">
                  Collectors can resell only where the royalty is paid; marketplaces that skip it cannot move
                  the tokens.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Panel>

          <Panel title="Launch phases" description="As the constructor took them, all closed." bodyClassName="px-6 py-2">
            <dl className="divide-y">
              {record.groups.map((group) => (
                <SummaryRow key={group.onchainId} label={`${group.onchainId}. ${group.name}`}>
                  <span className="numeric text-[0.8125rem]">
                    {formatNative(group.priceWei, chain.nativeSymbol, 18)} · {group.maxSupply.toLocaleString('en-US')}{' '}
                    tokens · closed
                  </span>
                </SummaryRow>
              ))}
            </dl>
          </Panel>
        </div>

        <Panel
          title="What happens next"
          description={artDeferred ? 'The art first, then Manage drop.' : 'All in Manage drop.'}
          bodyClassName="p-3"
        >
          <Stagger className="space-y-1">
            {nextSteps.map((step, index) => (
              <StaggerItem key={step.href}>
                <Link
                  href={step.href}
                  className="group hover:bg-surface-2 focus-visible:ring-ring flex items-start gap-3.5 rounded-lg p-3 outline-none transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2"
                >
                  <span
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    className="bg-surface-3 text-muted-foreground shadow-raised flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md [&_svg]:size-4"
                  >
                    {step.icon}
                  </span>
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <span className="block text-sm font-medium">
                      <span className="numeric text-muted-foreground mr-1.5">{index + 1}.</span>
                      {step.title}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground mt-0.5 block text-[0.8125rem] leading-snug">
                      {step.text}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <ArrowRightIcon
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    className="text-faint group-hover:text-foreground mt-2.5 size-4 shrink-0 transition-[transform,color] duration-300 group-hover:translate-x-0.5"
                  />
                </Link>
              </StaggerItem>
            ))}
          </Stagger>
        </Panel>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The flow**

Replace `apps/web/src/components/deploy/deploy-flow.tsx` with:

```tsx
'use client'

import { DEMO_COLLECTION, DEMO_DEPLOY_DRAFT, sampleSupply, toWei } from '@/lib/demo'
import { InfoIcon, RocketIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { useEffect, useMemo, useReducer, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { isAddress, type Address, type Hex } from 'viem'
import { useConfig } from 'wagmi'
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@cobweb/ui/components/alert'
import { Button } from '@cobweb/ui/components/button'
import { Input } from '@cobweb/ui/components/input'
import { Label } from '@cobweb/ui/components/label'
import { Panel } from '@cobweb/ui/components/panel'
import { StageFooter } from '@/components/shell/stage-footer'
import { NOT_A_HASH } from '@/lib/art/result'
import { chainView } from '@/lib/chain/chain-view'
import { slugState } from '@/lib/collections'
import type { Tables } from '@/lib/database.types'
import type { FeeSnapshot } from '@/lib/demo/types'
import type { DeployChain, DeployForm, DeployRecord, DoneJob, Prepared } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'
import type { FlowLink } from '@/lib/steps'
import { realDeployActions, type DeployActions } from './deploy-actions'
import { DeployDialog, type FinalCheck } from './deploy-dialog'
import { DeployForm as DeployFormPanel, royaltyBps, validateDeploy, type DeployFormValues } from './deploy-form'
import {
  browserStore,
  deployReducer,
  forgetDeploy,
  pendingDeploy,
  rememberDeploy,
  retryHash,
  runDeploy,
  type PendingDeploy,
} from './deploy-machine'
import { defaultPhases, DeployPhases, phaseErrors, type DraftPhase } from './deploy-phases'
import { DEPLOY_OUTCOMES, sampleDeployActions, useCreatorWallet, type DeployOutcome } from './deploy-sim'
import { DeploySummary, type CostState, type FeesState } from './deploy-summary'
import { DeployedView, SetUpDropButton } from './deployed-view'
import { isTxRunning, TX_IDLE, TxProgress } from './tx-progress'

export type DeployFlowProps = {
  collection: Tables<'collections'>
  chains: DeployChain[]
  /** The chain the fee was paid on (the done real job's quote chain); null when the creator picks. */
  fixedChainId: number | null
  /** The factory's fees on that chain, read on the server; the screen reads again in the browser. */
  initialFees: FeeSnapshot | null
  /** The job the contract starts on (null before any finished render), and the real set's job. */
  startJob: DoneJob | null
  realJob: DoneJob | null
  record: DeployRecord | null
  readOnly: boolean
  sample: boolean
  sampleOutcome?: string
  /** The session's wallet: the payout default, and the deployer the server prepares for. */
  sessionWallet: Address
  artSrc: string | null
  previous?: FlowLink
  next?: FlowLink
}

const FIELD_IDS = {
  chainId: '',
  payout: 'deploy-payout',
  royalty: 'deploy-royalty',
  royaltyReceiver: 'deploy-royalty-receiver',
  publicCap: 'deploy-cap',
  phases: 'deploy-phases',
}
const PREDICT_DEBOUNCE_MS = 400
const HASH_RE = /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/
const FORM_FIRST = 'Fill in the settings the contract was deployed with, then check the hash.'

/** The machine's form from the screen's values, or null while anything is invalid. */
function toDeployForm(
  values: DeployFormValues,
  phases: DraftPhase[],
  chainId: number | null,
  maxSupply: number,
  symbol: string,
): DeployForm | null {
  if (chainId === null) return null
  const bps = royaltyBps(values.royalty)
  if (bps === null || !isAddress(values.payout)) return null
  const receiver = values.sameReceiver ? values.payout : values.royaltyReceiver
  if (!isAddress(receiver)) return null
  const cap = Number(values.publicCap)
  if (!Number.isInteger(cap) || cap < 1 || cap > maxSupply) return null
  if (!phaseErrors(phases, maxSupply, symbol).ok) return null
  return {
    chainId,
    payout: values.payout as Address,
    royaltyBps: bps,
    royaltyReceiver: receiver as Address,
    publicMaxPerWallet: cap,
    groups: phases.map((phase) => ({
      name: phase.name.trim(),
      priceWei: toWei(phase.price.trim()),
      maxSupply: Number(phase.supply),
    })),
  }
}

/**
 * Deploy (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md, section 7.2): the form and the live summary with the
 * server's prediction, the final check and the transaction through the deploy machine, then the
 * deployed view from the record the server answers. Sample mode runs the same code on
 * `sampleDeployActions`.
 */
export function DeployFlow(props: DeployFlowProps) {
  const { collection, chains, fixedChainId, startJob, realJob, readOnly, sample, previous } = props
  const router = useRouter()
  const config = useConfig()
  const outcome = DEPLOY_OUTCOMES.find((entry) => entry === props.sampleOutcome) as DeployOutcome | undefined
  const wallet = useCreatorWallet(props.sessionWallet, sample)
  const walletRef = useRef(wallet)
  walletRef.current = wallet

  // Supply is set in Art (at Generate, or by the file count); a collection without it cannot deploy.
  const maxSupply = sampleSupply(collection.max_supply, DEMO_COLLECTION.maxSupply)
  // What the contract starts on and, when that is a placeholder, what it reveals to.
  const startsOn = startJob?.baseUri ?? null
  const startsOnPlaceholder = startJob !== null && startJob.placeholderImageCid !== null
  const revealsTo = startJob && realJob && startJob.id !== realJob.id ? realJob.baseUri : null

  const actions = useMemo<DeployActions>(
    () =>
      sample
        ? sampleDeployActions({
            collectionId: collection.id,
            collection: { name: collection.name, symbol: collection.symbol, maxSupply },
            wallet: wallet.address,
            baseUri: startsOn ?? '',
            revealsTo,
            outcome,
          })
        : realDeployActions(config, collection.id, () =>
            walletRef.current.connected ? walletRef.current.address : undefined,
          ),
    [sample, collection.id, collection.name, collection.symbol, maxSupply, wallet.address, startsOn, revealsTo, outcome, config],
  )

  // Payout and royalty recipient follow the wallet until the creator types their own.
  const [payout, setPayout] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [receiverText, setReceiverText] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    chainId: fixedChainId,
    royalty: String(DEMO_DEPLOY_DRAFT.royaltyBps / 100),
    sameReceiver: true,
    publicCap: String(DEMO_DEPLOY_DRAFT.publicMaxPerWallet),
  })
  const values: DeployFormValues = {
    ...form,
    payout: payout ?? wallet.address,
    royaltyReceiver: receiverText ?? wallet.address,
  }
  const [phases, setPhases] = useState<DraftPhase[]>(() => defaultPhases(maxSupply))
  const [touched, setTouched] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set())
  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)
  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [phase, dispatch] = useReducer(deployReducer, TX_IDLE)
  const [record, setRecord] = useState<DeployRecord | null>(props.record)
  const [justDeployed, setJustDeployed] = useState(false)
  // The mint page link as last reserved on this screen, for the summary and the deploy gate.
  const [address, setAddress] = useState({
    slug: collection.slug,
    slug_reserved_at: collection.slug_reserved_at,
  })
  const current = { ...collection, ...address }
  const link = slugState(current)
  // Required before deploying: a live reservation (or, for a deployed collection, its link).
  const linkSlug = link.kind === 'reserved' || link.kind === 'permanent' ? link.slug : null
  // The server's prediction for the current settings, refreshed 400 ms after the last change.
  const [prepared, setPrepared] = useState<Prepared | null>(null)
  const [predicting, setPredicting] = useState(false)
  const [fees, setFees] = useState<FeesState>(
    props.initialFees ? { status: 'ready', data: props.initialFees } : { status: 'loading', data: null },
  )
  const [feesAttempt, setFeesAttempt] = useState(0)
  const [cost, setCost] = useState<CostState>({ kind: 'none' })
  // A deploy this browser sent and the server has not recorded yet.
  const [resumable, setResumable] = useState<PendingDeploy | null>(null)
  const [hashOpen, setHashOpen] = useState(false)
  const [hashText, setHashText] = useState('')
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const running = isTxRunning(phase)

  const chainId = fixedChainId ?? values.chainId
  const chain = chains.find((entry) => entry.id === chainId) ?? null
  const symbol = chain?.nativeSymbol ?? 'ETH'
  const phaseCheck = phaseErrors(phases, maxSupply, symbol)
  const allErrors = validateDeploy(values, maxSupply)
  if (!phaseCheck.ok) allErrors.phases = phaseCheck.total ?? 'Check the phases.'
  const errors = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(allErrors).filter(([key]) => showAll || touched.has(key)),
  ) as typeof allErrors
  const valid = Object.keys(allErrors).length === 0
  const deployForm = valid ? toDeployForm(values, phases, chainId, maxSupply, symbol) : null
  const formKey = deployForm ? JSON.stringify(deployForm) : null
  const bps = royaltyBps(values.royalty)
  const payoutAddress = isAddress(values.payout) ? (values.payout as Address) : null
  const receiver = values.sameReceiver
    ? payoutAddress
    : isAddress(values.royaltyReceiver)
      ? (values.royaltyReceiver as Address)
      : null
  const walletChain = wallet.chainId === undefined ? undefined : chainView(wallet.chainId)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sample) setResumable(pendingDeploy(browserStore(), collection.id))
  }, [sample, collection.id])

  // The fees on the deploy chain, read in the browser; the server's snapshot stays shown meanwhile.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (chainId === null || record) return
    let live = true
    setFees((state) => ({ status: 'loading', data: state.data }))
    actions.fees(chainId, chain?.factory ?? null).then(
      (data) => {
        if (live) setFees({ status: 'ready', data })
      },
      () => {
        if (live) setFees({ status: 'error', data: null })
      },
    )
    return () => {
      live = false
    }
  }, [actions, chainId, chain?.factory, feesAttempt, record])

  // The server's prediction follows the form, 400 ms after the last change: the same action the deploy runs first.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (readOnly || record || formKey === null) {
      setPrepared(null)
      setPredicting(false)
      return
    }
    let live = true
    setPredicting(true)
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      actions.prepare(JSON.parse(formKey) as DeployForm).then(
        (result) => {
          if (!live) return
          setPrepared(result.ok ? result.data : null)
          setPredicting(false)
        },
        () => {
          if (!live) return
          setPrepared(null)
          setPredicting(false)
        },
      )
    }, PREDICT_DEBOUNCE_MS)
    return () => {
      live = false
      clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [actions, readOnly, record, formKey])

  // The deploy cost, while the wallet is connected and on the deploy chain (sample mode always has one).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!prepared) {
      setCost({ kind: 'none' })
      return
    }
    if (!sample && !wallet.connected) {
      setCost({ kind: 'no-wallet' })
      return
    }
    if (!sample && wallet.chainId !== prepared.chainId) {
      setCost({ kind: 'other-chain', chainName: chainView(prepared.chainId).name })
      return
    }
    let live = true
    setCost({ kind: 'loading' })
    actions.estimateCost(prepared).then(
      (wei) => {
        if (live) setCost(wei ? { kind: 'ready', wei } : { kind: 'unknown' })
      },
      () => {
        if (live) setCost({ kind: 'unknown' })
      },
    )
    return () => {
      live = false
    }
  }, [actions, prepared, sample, wallet.connected, wallet.chainId])

  const ready =
    valid &&
    maxSupply > 0 &&
    !!linkSlug &&
    !!deployForm &&
    !!prepared &&
    !!chain &&
    fees.status === 'ready' &&
    !!fees.data &&
    (sample || wallet.connected)

  function change(patch: Partial<DeployFormValues>) {
    const { payout: nextPayout, royaltyReceiver: nextReceiver, ...rest } = patch
    if (nextPayout !== undefined) setPayout(nextPayout)
    if (nextReceiver !== undefined) setReceiverText(nextReceiver)
    setForm((state) => ({ ...state, ...rest }))
    setTouched((state) => new Set([...state, ...Object.keys(patch)]))
    if (patch.chainId !== undefined) dispatch({ type: 'set', phase: TX_IDLE })
  }

  function openFinalCheck() {
    if (!valid) {
      setShowAll(true)
      // Take the creator to the first field that needs attention.
      const first = Object.keys(allErrors)[0] as keyof typeof FIELD_IDS
      const target =
        first === 'chainId'
          ? document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="choice-card"]')
          : document.getElementById(FIELD_IDS[first])
      target?.focus()
      return
    }
    if (phase.step === 'failed') dispatch({ type: 'set', phase: TX_IDLE })
    setDialogOpen(true)
  }

  /** Deploys with the current form, or checks a deploy already sent (`sent`): the machine does the rest. */
  async function deploy(sent?: PendingDeploy) {
    const input = sent?.form ?? deployForm
    if (!input || (!sent && !ready)) return
    setNotice(null)
    const store = browserStore()
    const deployChainId = input.chainId
    const result = await runDeploy(
      {
        form: input,
        account: sample || wallet.connected ? wallet.address : undefined,
        connectedChainId: wallet.chainId,
        sentHash: sent?.hash,
      },
      {
        prepare: (form) => actions.prepare(form),
        switchChain: (id) => actions.switchChain(id),
        simulate: (ready) => actions.simulate(ready),
        wait: (hash) => actions.wait(hash, deployChainId),
        confirm: (hash, form) => actions.confirm(hash, form),
        remember: (hash, form) => {
          if (!sample) rememberDeploy(store, collection.id, { hash, form })
        },
        forget: () => {
          if (!sample) forgetDeploy(store, collection.id)
        },
        sleep: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
        now: () => Date.now(),
      },
      dispatch,
    )
    if (!sample) setResumable(pendingDeploy(store, collection.id))
    if (!result) return
    setRecord(result)
    setJustDeployed(true)
    setDialogOpen(false)
    // The collection is deployed: the stepper and Manage drop open on the next render.
    if (!sample) router.refresh()
  }

  /** "Try again" in the dialog: a deploy sent but not confirmed is checked, not sent again. */
  function tryAgain() {
    const hash = retryHash(phase)
    if (hash) {
      const pending = resumable?.hash === hash ? resumable : deployForm ? { hash, form: deployForm } : null
      if (pending) return deploy(pending)
    }
    return deploy()
  }

  function checkTyped() {
    const text = hashText.trim()
    if (!HASH_RE.test(text)) return setNotice(NOT_A_HASH)
    if (!deployForm) return setNotice(FORM_FIRST)
    const pending = { hash: text as Hex, form: deployForm }
    rememberDeploy(browserStore(), collection.id, pending)
    setResumable(pending)
    return deploy(pending)
  }

  function discard() {
    forgetDeploy(browserStore(), collection.id)
    setResumable(null)
    setNotice(null)
    dispatch({ type: 'set', phase: TX_IDLE })
  }

  if (record) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
        <div className="pb-10">
          <DeployedView
            collection={current}
            record={record}
            chain={chainView(record.chainId)}
            artSrc={props.artSrc}
            focusOnMount={justDeployed}
          />
        </div>
        {/* Continue and the primary action both lead to Manage drop: one button is enough. */}
        <StageFooter previous={previous}>
          <SetUpDropButton collectionId={collection.id} />
        </StageFooter>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const check: FinalCheck | null =
    ready && deployForm && prepared && chain && fees.data && linkSlug
      ? {
          name: collection.name,
          mintPageLink: `cobweb.app/${linkSlug}`,
          symbol: collection.symbol,
          maxSupply,
          chain,
          walletChain,
          predicted: prepared.predicted,
          payout: deployForm.payout,
          royaltyBps: deployForm.royaltyBps,
          royaltyReceiver: deployForm.royaltyReceiver,
          publicCap: deployForm.publicMaxPerWallet,
          phases: deployForm.groups,
          fees: fees.data,
          deployCostWei: cost.kind === 'ready' ? cost.wei : undefined,
        }
      : null

  const hint =
    maxSupply === 0
      ? 'Set the supply in Art first.'
      : !startsOn
        ? 'Render the art before deploying.'
        : !linkSlug
          ? 'Reserve your mint page link first.'
          : !chain
            ? 'Choose a chain to deploy on.'
            : fees.status === 'loading' && !fees.data
              ? 'Reading fees from the factory.'
              : fees.status === 'error'
                ? 'Fees could not be read.'
                : !sample && !wallet.connected
                  ? 'Connect your wallet to deploy.'
                  : null
  const checking = !dialogOpen && phase.step !== 'idle'

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
      <div className="grid items-start gap-8 pb-10 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_22rem] xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_25rem]">
        <div className="space-y-8">
          <DeployFormPanel
            collection={collection}
            chains={chains}
            fixedChainId={fixedChainId}
            walletAddress={wallet.address}
            walletChainId={wallet.chainId}
            values={values}
            errors={errors}
            readOnly={readOnly}
            onChange={change}
            onAddressSaved={setAddress}
          />
          <DeployPhases
            phases={phases}
            onChange={(nextPhases) => {
              setPhases(nextPhases)
              setTouched((state) => new Set([...state, 'phases']))
            }}
            maxSupply={maxSupply}
            chain={chain}
            readOnly={readOnly}
            showErrors={showAll || touched.has('phases')}
          />
          {!readOnly && !sample && (
            <Panel
              title="Sent from elsewhere?"
              description="A deploy sent outside this page, or one this page lost, is recorded from its transaction hash. Cobweb checks it against the settings above."
            >
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {resumable && phase.step === 'idle' && (
                  <Alert>
                    <InfoIcon aria-hidden="true" />
                    <AlertTitle>A deploy is waiting to be checked</AlertTitle>
                    <AlertDescription>
                      <p>
                        This browser sent a deploy for this collection that Cobweb has not recorded yet. Check it
                        before you deploy again.
                      </p>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => void deploy(resumable)} disabled={running}>
                          Check the deploy
                        </Button>
                        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={discard} disabled={running}>
                          Discard this attempt
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </AlertDescription>
                  </Alert>
                )}
                {checking && (
                  <TxProgress
                    phase={phase}
                    txUrl={chain?.explorerUrl ? (hash) => `${chain.explorerUrl}/tx/${hash}` : undefined}
                    steps={[
                      {
                        step: 'verifying',
                        label: 'Recording the contract',
                        detail: 'Cobweb reads the factory event from the receipt and records the address once it equals the prediction.',
                      },
                    ]}
                  />
                )}
                {hashOpen ? (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <Label htmlFor="deploy-hash">Transaction hash</Label>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <Input
                        id="deploy-hash"
                        value={hashText}
                        onChange={(event) => setHashText(event.target.value)}
                        placeholder="0x"
                        spellCheck={false}
                        className="numeric max-w-xl min-w-0 flex-1"
                        disabled={running}
                        aria-invalid={notice === NOT_A_HASH}
                        aria-describedby="deploy-hash-help"
                      />
                      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={checkTyped} disabled={running || !hashText.trim()}>
                        Check this hash
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                    <p id="deploy-hash-help" className="text-muted-foreground text-[0.8125rem] leading-snug">
                      Use this if you deployed and this page lost the transaction. The settings above must be the
                      ones the contract was deployed with.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <Button variant="link" className="h-auto p-0" onClick={() => setHashOpen(true)}>
                    I have a transaction hash
                  </Button>
                )}
                {notice && <p className="text-destructive-foreground text-[0.8125rem]">{notice}</p>}
              </div>
            </Panel>
          )}
        </div>
        <aside aria-label="Deploy summary" className="lg:sticky lg:top-6">
          <DeploySummary
            collection={current}
            chain={chain}
            fees={fees}
            onRetryFees={() => setFeesAttempt((count) => count + 1)}
            cost={cost}
            predicted={prepared?.predicted ?? null}
            predicting={predicting}
            payout={payoutAddress}
            royaltyBps={bps}
            royaltyReceiver={receiver}
            startsOn={startsOn}
            startsOnPlaceholder={startsOnPlaceholder}
            revealsTo={revealsTo}
            artSrc={props.artSrc}
          />
        </aside>
      </div>

      <StageFooter previous={previous}>
        {hint && <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">{hint}</p>}
        {!readOnly && (
          <Button
            onClick={openFinalCheck}
            disabled={maxSupply === 0 || !startsOn || !linkSlug || !chain || fees.status !== 'ready' || (!sample && !wallet.connected)}
          >
            <RocketIcon />
            Deploy contract
          </Button>
        )}
      </StageFooter>

      <DeployDialog open={dialogOpen} onOpenChange={setDialogOpen} check={check} phase={phase} onDeploy={() => void tryAgain()} />
    </div>
  )
}
```

One point on the footer: before the deploy lands there is no Continue (Manage drop is locked), so the footer shows Back, the hint and the Deploy button, as today; once deployed, Back and "Set up your drop". The `next` prop stays on `DeployFlowProps` for the page's sake and is not read by the flow.

- [ ] **Step 7: The screen and the page**

Replace `apps/web/src/components/deploy/deploy-screen.tsx` with:

```tsx
import type { Address } from 'viem'
import type { Tables } from '@/lib/database.types'
import {
  DEMO_CREATOR,
  DEMO_DEFERRED_RENDER_JOBS,
  DEMO_DEPLOYMENT,
  DEMO_MINT_GROUPS,
  DEMO_PUBLIC_MAX_PER_WALLET,
  DEMO_RENDER_JOBS,
} from '@/lib/demo'
import type { DeployChain, DeployRecord, DeployView } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'
import type { FlowLink } from '@/lib/steps'
import { ArtDeferredProvider } from './art-deferred'
import { DeployFlow } from './deploy-flow'

export type DeployScreenProps = {
  collection: Tables<'collections'>
  chains: DeployChain[]
  /** Real mode: what the server read. Null in sample mode. */
  view: DeployView | null
  sample: boolean
  /** Sample mode: `?state=deployed` shows the deployed result instead of the form. */
  sampleDeployed: boolean
  readOnly: boolean
  /** The session's wallet, checksummed. */
  sessionWallet: Address
  /** The flow screens either side, for the footer the screen renders itself. */
  previous?: FlowLink
  next?: FlowLink
  /**
   * Sample mode only (`?outcome=`): makes the first attempt fail, so failure states can be
   * reviewed. rejected | reverted | balance | switch-rejected | fees-error.
   */
  sampleOutcome?: string
  /** "Finish the art later" (MASTER_PLAN, Flow: pre-reveal): the contract starts on the placeholder. */
  artDeferred?: boolean
}

/** The sample's deployment record: the Night Owls deployment on Base, as `?state=deployed` shows it. */
export function sampleRecord(): DeployRecord {
  return {
    chainId: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.chainId,
    contractAddress: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.contractAddress,
    txHash: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.txHash,
    deployer: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.deployer,
    deployedAt: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.deployedAt,
    baseUri: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.baseUri,
    payout: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.payoutAddress,
    royaltyBps: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.royaltyBps,
    royaltyReceiver: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.royaltyReceiver,
    publicMaxPerWallet: DEMO_PUBLIC_MAX_PER_WALLET,
    fees: DEMO_DEPLOYMENT.fees,
    groups: DEMO_MINT_GROUPS.map((group) => ({
      onchainId: group.id,
      name: group.name,
      priceWei: group.priceWei,
      maxSupply: group.maxSupply,
    })),
  }
}

/**
 * Deploy: the server part. In real mode it hands the flow what `getDeployView` read (the start and
 * real jobs, the paid chain, the fee snapshot, the record). In sample mode the contract starts on the
 * sample placeholder job ("Reveal later") and reveals to the real set, or, with deferred art, has
 * nothing to reveal to yet; `?state=deployed` shows the sample record.
 */
export function DeployScreen({ artDeferred = false, ...props }: DeployScreenProps) {
  const { collection, sample, view } = props
  if (sample) {
    const start = DEMO_DEFERRED_RENDER_JOBS.done
    const real = artDeferred ? null : DEMO_RENDER_JOBS.done
    return (
      <ArtDeferredProvider value={artDeferred}>
        <DeployFlow
          collection={collection}
          chains={props.chains}
          fixedChainId={null}
          initialFees={null}
          startJob={start}
          realJob={real}
          record={props.sampleDeployed ? sampleRecord() : null}
          readOnly={props.readOnly}
          sample
          sampleOutcome={props.sampleOutcome}
          sessionWallet={DEMO_CREATOR}
          artSrc={start.placeholder ? start.placeholder.imageUrl : '/demo/tokens/1.svg'}
          previous={props.previous}
          next={props.next}
        />
      </ArtDeferredProvider>
    )
  }
  if (!view) throw new Error('The deploy view is missing.')
  const start = view.jobs.start
  // The placeholder's served file when the contract starts on it, the collage when the real job has one.
  const artSrc =
    start && start.placeholderImageCid !== null && start.placeholder
      ? start.placeholder.imageUrl
      : view.jobs.real?.collageKey
        ? `/api/collections/${collection.id}/collage`
        : null
  return (
    <ArtDeferredProvider value={artDeferred}>
      <DeployFlow
        collection={collection}
        chains={props.chains}
        fixedChainId={view.chainId}
        initialFees={view.fees}
        startJob={start}
        realJob={view.jobs.real}
        record={view.record}
        readOnly={props.readOnly}
        sample={false}
        sessionWallet={props.sessionWallet}
        artSrc={artSrc}
        previous={props.previous}
        next={props.next}
      />
    </ArtDeferredProvider>
  )
}
```

Replace `apps/web/src/app/collections/[id]/deploy/page.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { getAddress } from 'viem'
import { getDeployView } from '@/app/actions/deploy'
import { DeployScreen } from '@/components/deploy/deploy-screen'
import { StagePage } from '@/components/shell/stage-page'
import { requireOwnedCollection, requireUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import { deployChains } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-core'
import type { DeployView } from '@/lib/deploy/deploy-types'
import {
  LockedStagePage,
  placeholderReady,
  searchParam,
  stageContext,
  type CollectionParams,
  type SearchParams,
} from '../_lib/stage'

/**
 * Stage 4, Deploy (docs/specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md). The server prepares the constructor from the
 * collection and the finished render, the wallet deploys through the factory, and the server records
 * the contract from the factory's event. Sample mode only (`NEXT_PUBLIC_DEMO_DATA=1`):
 * `?state=deployed` shows the deployed result instead of the form,
 * `?outcome=rejected|reverted|balance|switch-rejected|fees-error` makes the first attempt fail, and
 * `?art=deferred` deploys on the placeholder of the deferred-art scenario (MASTER_PLAN, Flow: pre-reveal).
 */
export default async function DeployPage({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: CollectionParams
  searchParams: SearchParams
}) {
  const { id } = await params
  const collection = await requireOwnedCollection(id)
  const user = await requireUser()
  // A deferred collection's Deploy opens once its placeholder job is done, with the collection still a draft.
  const ready = await placeholderReady(collection)
  const ctx = stageContext(
    collection,
    'deploy',
    'deploy',
    { art: await searchParam(searchParams, 'art') },
    { placeholderReady: ready },
  )
  if (ctx.access === 'locked') return <LockedStagePage collection={collection} stage="deploy" />

  const sampleDeployed = ctx.demo && (await searchParam(searchParams, 'state')) === 'deployed'
  let view: DeployView | null = null
  if (!ctx.demo) {
    const result = await getDeployView(collection.id)
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.error)
    view = result.data
  }
  const deployed = ctx.demo ? sampleDeployed : view !== null && view.record !== null

  return (
    <StagePage
      // Once deployed there is no stepper, so no "Stage 4 of 4" either: this is the receipt.
      eyebrow={deployed ? undefined : ctx.eyebrow}
      title="Deploy"
      description={
        deployed
          ? 'The contract is live. Its receipt, and where to go next.'
          : 'Set royalties, the payout address and the launch phases, and deploy the contract from your wallet.'
      }
      width="wide"
      bodyHasFooter
    >
      {/* The screen renders the footer: Deploy is its action, and Manage drop opens once deployed. */}
      <DeployScreen
        collection={collection}
        chains={deployChains()}
        view={view}
        sample={ctx.demo}
        sampleDeployed={sampleDeployed}
        readOnly={ctx.readOnly}
        artDeferred={ctx.artDeferred}
        sessionWallet={getAddress(user.walletAddress)}
        previous={ctx.previous}
        next={ctx.next}
        sampleOutcome={ctx.demo ? await searchParam(searchParams, 'outcome') : undefined}
      />
    </StagePage>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The docs**

In `docs/MASTER_PLAN.md`:

1. Under `### Contracts` in `## Decisions`, after the line that starts `- **Royalty** is per collection.`, add:

```markdown
- **The shipped collection artifact** (decided 14 October 2026; [specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md](specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md)). The web app deploys the creation code it ships from `@cobweb/shared/collection`, generated from the Foundry build by the copy script with its keccak256 hash and the ABI, never fetched at request time. `foundry.toml` pins `evm_version`, the shared package's test asserts the shipped hash against `contracts/out`, and CI runs that test after `forge build`, so the hash the admin approves on the factory is the hash the web app sends.
```

2. Under `### Flow` in `## Decisions`, after the line that starts `- **Deploy defaults.**`, add:

```markdown
- **The deploy chain** (decided 14 October 2026; [specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md](specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md)). The contract lives on the chain the art fee was paid on: when the collection has a done real job, the deploy chain is that job's `quote_chain_id` and the wallet switches to it; a deferred collection, which has paid nothing, picks its chain at deploy, and the real art's fee is quoted on it later because the lock reads `collections.chain_id` once set. `collections.chain_id` is written at deploy and never changes.
- **Prepare and prove** (decided 14 October 2026; [specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md](specs/2026-10-14-deploy.md)). A read-only action builds the constructor from the collection row, the finished render and the form, encodes it, picks the salt (the collection id's 16 bytes, left-aligned in 32) and predicts the CREATE2 address; the browser simulates and sends the factory call with those bytes; the confirming action rebuilds the same arguments, decodes the factory's `CollectionDeployed` log from the receipt and records the contract only when the event's address equals the prediction. Nothing is stored before the receipt. One `deployments` row per collection holds what was sent and what landed; the launch phases become `mint_groups` rows; `collection_record_deploy` writes all three. The name, symbol and description freeze at lock, since the metadata the worker writes and the contract's name must agree.
```
